⬡ads_accounts

Connected ad platform accounts. One row per linked ad account (Google Ads, Meta, LinkedIn, Microsoft, TikTok). Contains account name, currency (fixed at creation), timezone, manager hierarchy. Source: pulled from each platform API on connect and refreshed daily.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, platform
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, linkedin, microsoft, tiktok.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID. Google CID, Meta ad account ID, LinkedIn ad account URN, etc.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline — ties this ad account to the ListenLayer account.
account_nameSTRINGDisplay name of the ad account as set in the platform.
currency_codeSTRINGISO 4217 currency code (USD, EUR, GBP). Fixed per account at creation — cannot be changed. All monetary values for this account are in this currency.
timezoneSTRINGAccount timezone (e.g., America/New_York). Used by the platform for day boundaries in reporting.
is_managerBOOLTRUE if this is a manager/parent account (Google MCC, Meta Business Manager). Manager accounts contain child ad accounts.
parent_account_idSTRINGFor child accounts under a manager: the parent manager account ID. Establishes MCC → client account hierarchy. NULL for top-level accounts.
statusSTRINGAccount status: active, paused, disabled, closed. Normalized across platforms.
connected_atTIMESTAMPWhen this ad account was connected to ListenLayer. Set once on initial connection.
custom_fieldsJSONPlatform-specific fields that do not map to standard columns. Query via JSON_VALUE(custom_fields, "$.field_name").
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.

⬡ads_campaigns

Ad campaigns across all connected platforms. One row per campaign. Pacing STRUCT tracks budget utilization (spend vs budget, pacing ratio, impression loss). Health STRUCT tracks activity state and CPC/CTR trends. Intelligence STRUCT classifies campaign goal and assesses performance. Source: pulled daily from platform APIs, computed fields updated on each sync.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, platform, campaign_id
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, linkedin, microsoft, tiktok.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID. FK → ads_accounts.platform_account_id.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
campaign_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform campaign ID. Unique within (platform, account_id).
campaign_nameSTRINGCampaign display name as set in the platform.
platform_entity_typeSTRINGWhat the platform calls this entity. "Campaign" for Google/Meta/Microsoft/TikTok, "Campaign Group" for LinkedIn.
statusSTRINGCampaign status: ENABLED, PAUSED, REMOVED, ARCHIVED. Normalized across platforms.
campaign_typeSTRINGWhat kind of campaign this is — unified across platforms. Google: SEARCH, DISPLAY, VIDEO, SHOPPING, PERFORMANCE_MAX. Meta: AUCTION, RESERVED. LinkedIn/TikTok: derived from objective. Normalized by our pipeline.
objectiveSTRINGCampaign objective. Meta: CONVERSIONS, TRAFFIC, AWARENESS, REACH. LinkedIn: BRAND_AWARENESS, LEAD_GENERATION, WEBSITE_VISITS. TikTok: CONVERSIONS, TRAFFIC. Google: inferred from channel_type. NULL if platform does not expose.
bidding_strategy_typeSTRINGBidding strategy: TARGET_CPA, TARGET_ROAS, MAXIMIZE_CONVERSIONS, LOWEST_COST, etc. Platform-specific values.
budget_amountFLOAT64Budget amount in account currency (see ads_accounts.currency_code). Interpretation depends on budget_type (daily vs lifetime).
budget_typeSTRINGBudget type: daily, lifetime, no_limit. Determines how budget_amount is interpreted.
start_dateDATECampaign start date. NULL if not set (runs immediately on enable).
end_dateDATECampaign end date. NULL if no end date (runs until paused or budget exhausted).
pacing.spend_to_dateFLOAT64Cumulative spend since start_date or first spend date, in account currency.
pacing.budget_remainingFLOAT64budget_amount - spend_to_date. For lifetime budgets only. NULL if daily budget or no_limit.
pacing.pacing_ratioFLOAT64Core pacing metric. Daily: today_spend / daily_budget. Lifetime: spend_to_date / budget_amount. 0.85 = 85% utilized. NULL if no budget.
pacing.pacing_statusSTRINGDerived from pacing_ratio: on_track, underpacing, overpacing, exhausted, no_budget.
pacing.avg_daily_spendFLOAT647-day average daily spend in account currency. Smoothed rate for trend analysis.
pacing.target_daily_spendFLOAT64For lifetime budgets with end_date: budget_remaining / days_remaining. What needs to be spent per day to finish on budget. NULL for daily budgets or no end_date.
pacing.impression_lost_to_budget_7dFLOAT647-day average of impression_lost_to_budget_pct from ads_metrics_daily. Google and Microsoft only. NULL for Meta, LinkedIn, TikTok.
pacing.last_spend_dateDATEMost recent date with spend > 0. NULL if campaign has never spent.
pacing.days_since_spendINT64Days since last_spend_date. 0 = spent today, 5 = no spend in 5 days.
pacing.computed_atTIMESTAMPWhen pacing was last computed.
health.activity_statusSTRINGCampaign activity state: active, stalled, new, winding_down, ended. Derived from platform status, spend recency, and campaign dates.
health.days_activeINT64Days since start_date or first spend date.
health.has_conversionsBOOLTRUE if any platform_conversions > 0 in the last 30 days.
health.cpc_trendSTRINGCost per click trend: improving (CPC decreasing), stable, degrading (CPC increasing). Compares 7-day average to prior 7-day average.
health.ctr_trendSTRINGClick-through rate trend: improving (CTR increasing), stable, degrading (CTR decreasing). Compares 7-day average to prior 7-day average.
health.computed_atTIMESTAMPWhen health was last computed.
intelligence.goalSTRINGAI-assessed campaign goal: revenue, leads, traffic, awareness, retargeting, engagement, app_installs.
intelligence.categorySTRINGAI-assessed campaign category: brand, prospecting, retargeting, nurture, competitive, awareness, lead_gen.
intelligence.subcategorySTRINGAI-assessed subcategory: cold_audience, warm_audience, lookalike, remarketing_cart, brand_terms, competitor_terms, generic_terms, etc.
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64AI confidence in the classification (0.0-1.0).
intelligence.last_analyzed_atTIMESTAMPWhen the AI last classified this campaign.
custom_fieldsJSONPlatform-specific fields that do not map to standard columns. Meta: special_ad_categories, campaign_budget_optimization. Google: advertising_channel_sub_type, channel_type. Query via JSON_VALUE(custom_fields, "$.field_name").
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen the campaign was created in the ad platform.
updated_atTIMESTAMPWhen the campaign was last modified in the ad platform.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.

⬡ads_ad_groups

Ad groups (Google/Microsoft), ad sets (Meta), or campaigns (LinkedIn) — the targeting-level entity below Campaign. Contains targeting config (audiences, demographics as JSON), bid strategy, optimization goal. Source: pulled daily from platform APIs.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, platform, campaign_id, ad_group_id
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, linkedin, microsoft, tiktok.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID. FK → ads_accounts.platform_account_id.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
campaign_idSTRING NOT NULLParent campaign ID. FK → ads_campaigns.campaign_id.
ad_group_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad group ID. Unique within (platform, account_id, campaign_id).
ad_group_nameSTRINGAd group display name as set in the platform.
platform_entity_typeSTRINGWhat the platform calls this entity. "Ad Group" for Google/Microsoft/TikTok, "Ad Set" for Meta, "Campaign" for LinkedIn.
statusSTRINGAd group status: ENABLED, PAUSED, REMOVED, ARCHIVED. Normalized across platforms.
ad_group_typeSTRINGAd group type — normalized across platforms. Google/Microsoft: SEARCH_STANDARD, DISPLAY_STANDARD, SHOPPING_PRODUCT_ADS. Meta: derived from campaign objective. LinkedIn/TikTok: derived from campaign type. NULL if not applicable.
optimization_goalSTRINGOptimization goal for Meta/TikTok: CONVERSIONS, LINK_CLICKS, IMPRESSIONS, REACH, etc. Determines what the platform optimizes delivery for. NULL for Google/Microsoft (set at campaign level).
bid_strategySTRINGAd group level bid strategy. Overrides campaign-level strategy when set. Platform-specific values.
bid_amountFLOAT64Ad group level bid amount in account currency. NULL if bidding is fully automated or set at campaign level.
targetingJSONFull targeting configuration as a JSON object. Contains audience segments, geo targeting, demographics, interests, placements, device targeting, and custom audiences. Structure varies by platform.
custom_fieldsJSONPlatform-specific fields that do not map to standard columns. Meta: attribution_spec, audience_expansion_enabled. LinkedIn: audience_expansion_enabled. Google: cpc_bid_micros. Query via JSON_VALUE(custom_fields, "$.field_name").
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen the ad group was created in the ad platform.
updated_atTIMESTAMPWhen the ad group was last modified in the ad platform.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.

⬡ads_metrics_daily

Daily aggregated performance metrics across all ad platforms. Normalized destination_clicks comparable across platforms plus platform-specific metrics. Video and engagement STRUCTs. Source: pulled daily with platform-specific lookback re-pull (3d Google/Microsoft, 7d TikTok, 28d Meta). Used for spend reporting, cross-platform comparison, ROAS.

Partition: dateCluster: platform, account_id, campaign_id
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, microsoft, linkedin, tiktok.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID (e.g., Google customer ID, Meta ad account ID).
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID that owns this ad account.
dateDATE NOT NULLThe reporting date for these metrics. Partition key.
campaign_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform campaign ID.
ad_group_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad group ID (ad set on Meta, ad group on Google/MSFT/LinkedIn/TikTok).
ad_idSTRINGPlatform ad/creative ID. NULL when metrics are reported at the ad group level only.
currency_codeSTRING NOT NULLISO 4217 currency code of the ad account (e.g., USD, EUR, GBP).
impressionsINT64Number of times the ad was shown.
destination_clicksINT64Clicks that send users to a destination URL. Normalized across platforms: link clicks (Meta), headline clicks (LinkedIn), clicks (Google/MSFT), landing page clicks (TikTok). Use this for cross-platform comparison.
platform_total_clicksINT64Raw total clicks as reported by the platform. May include profile clicks, sitelink clicks, call clicks, etc. Not comparable across platforms.
spendFLOAT64Amount spent in the ad account currency. See currency_code for the denomination.
spend_usdFLOAT64Amount spent converted to USD. NULL if currency conversion is unavailable.
platform_conversionsFLOAT64Conversion count as reported by the platform. FLOAT64 because some platforms report fractional/modeled conversions.
platform_conversion_valueFLOAT64Total conversion value as reported by the platform, in account currency.
networkSTRINGAd delivery network. Google/MSFT: g (search), s (search partner), d (display), a (audience). TikTok: tiktok, pangle. NULL for Meta and LinkedIn.
search_impression_shareFLOAT64Fraction of eligible impressions received (0.0–1.0). Google and Microsoft search campaigns only.
impression_lost_to_budget_pctFLOAT64Estimated fraction of impressions lost due to insufficient budget (0.0–1.0). Google and Microsoft only.
impression_lost_to_rank_pctFLOAT64Estimated fraction of impressions lost due to ad rank (0.0–1.0). Google and Microsoft only.
top_impression_rate_pctFLOAT64Fraction of impressions shown in top positions above organic results (0.0–1.0). Google and Microsoft only.
reachINT64Number of unique users who saw the ad. Primarily Meta and TikTok. NULL when the platform does not report reach.
frequencyFLOAT64Average number of times each unique user saw the ad (impressions / reach). Primarily Meta and TikTok.
purchasesINT64Meta Ads: number of purchase conversion events. NULL for non-Meta platforms.
purchase_valueFLOAT64Meta Ads: total value of purchase conversions in account currency. NULL for non-Meta platforms.
leadsINT64Meta Ads: number of lead conversion events. NULL for non-Meta platforms.
add_to_cartINT64Meta Ads: number of add-to-cart conversion events. NULL for non-Meta platforms.
purchase_roasFLOAT64Meta Ads: purchase return on ad spend (purchase_value / spend). NULL for non-Meta platforms.
video.playsINT64Total video plays/starts.
video.watched_2sINT64Video plays that reached 2 seconds (Meta continuous video plays, TikTok 2s views).
video.watched_6sINT64Video plays that reached 6 seconds (TikTok 6s focused views). NULL on platforms without this metric.
video.views_p25INT64Video plays that reached 25% completion.
video.views_p50INT64Video plays that reached 50% completion.
video.views_p75INT64Video plays that reached 75% completion.
video.views_p100INT64Video plays that reached 100% completion.
video.avg_play_secondsFLOAT64Average video play duration in seconds.
engagement.totalINT64Total engagements (reactions + comments + shares + other interactions).
engagement.likesINT64Likes/reactions on the ad.
engagement.commentsINT64Comments on the ad.
engagement.sharesINT64Shares/reposts of the ad.
engagement.followsINT64Follows generated by the ad (TikTok, LinkedIn). NULL on platforms without this metric.
breakdownsJSONPlatform-reported performance breakdowns as schemaless JSON. Meta: placement, age_gender, country. Google: device, network. Microsoft: device, network. TikTok: placement. Each breakdown is an array of objects with the dimension value plus metrics.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline wrote this row.

⬡ads_keywords

Search keywords targeted by Google Ads and Microsoft Ads. Contains keyword text, match type (exact/phrase/broad), quality score (1-10), bid amount. Other platforms do not have keyword targeting. Source: pulled daily from Google/Microsoft APIs.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, platform, ad_group_id
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, microsoft. Only search platforms have keywords.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID. FK → ads_accounts.platform_account_id.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
campaign_idSTRING NOT NULLParent campaign ID. FK → ads_campaigns.campaign_id.
ad_group_idSTRING NOT NULLParent ad group ID. FK → ads_ad_groups.ad_group_id.
keyword_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform keyword criterion ID. Unique within (platform, account_id, ad_group_id).
keyword_textSTRINGThe keyword text (e.g., "running shoes", "best crm software"). The actual search term or phrase being bid on.
match_typeSTRINGKeyword match type: EXACT, PHRASE, BROAD. Determines how broadly the keyword matches user search queries.
statusSTRINGKeyword status: ENABLED, PAUSED, REMOVED. Normalized across platforms.
quality_scoreINT64Platform quality score (1-10). Measures ad relevance, expected CTR, and landing page experience. NULL when not yet computed or insufficient data.
bid_amountFLOAT64Keyword-level bid amount in account currency. Overrides ad group bid when set. NULL if using ad group or campaign level bidding.
health.quality_score_currentINT64Latest quality score snapshot denormalized from most recent ads_keyword_performance row. Saves a JOIN for dashboards.
health.quality_score_trendSTRINGQuality score trend: improving, stable, degrading. Compares current quality score to 30 days ago.
health.is_activeBOOLTRUE if the keyword has impressions in the last 7 days.
health.days_since_impressionINT64Days since last impression. High values indicate the keyword may be too narrow, paused, or outbid.
health.computed_atTIMESTAMPWhen health was last computed.
custom_fieldsJSONPlatform-specific fields that do not map to standard columns. Query via JSON_VALUE(custom_fields, "$.field_name").
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen the keyword was created in the ad platform.
updated_atTIMESTAMPWhen the keyword was last modified in the ad platform.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.

⬡ads_keyword_performance

Weekly performance snapshots per search keyword. Google and Microsoft only. Tracks quality score trending, impression share, bid estimates. Google STRUCT includes QS subcomponents (expected CTR, ad relevance, landing page experience). Source: aggregated weekly from Google/Microsoft keyword reports.

Partition: dateCluster: platform, account_id, ad_group_id
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google or microsoft. Only search platforms have keyword-level data.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID. FK → ads_accounts.platform_account_id.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID that owns this ad account.
campaign_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform campaign ID. FK -> ads_campaigns.campaign_id.
ad_group_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad group ID. FK -> ads_ad_groups.ad_group_id.
keyword_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform keyword criterion ID. FK -> ads_keywords.keyword_id.
dateDATE NOT NULLDate of the performance metrics. Partition key.
keyword_textSTRINGThe keyword text as configured in the ad platform.
match_typeSTRINGKeyword match type: EXACT, PHRASE, BROAD. Normalized across Google and Microsoft.
impressionsINT64Number of times ads triggered by this keyword were shown during the snapshot week.
destination_clicksINT64Clicks that sent users to a destination URL during the snapshot week.
spendFLOAT64Amount spent on this keyword during the snapshot week, in account currency.
ctrFLOAT64Click-through rate for the snapshot week (destination_clicks / impressions).
cpcFLOAT64Cost per click for the snapshot week (spend / destination_clicks).
platform_conversionsFLOAT64Conversion count as reported by the platform. FLOAT64 because some platforms report fractional/modeled conversions.
google.quality_scoreINT64Quality score snapshot (1-10). Reflects ad relevance, expected CTR, and landing page experience.
google.expected_ctrSTRINGExpected CTR rating: ABOVE_AVERAGE, AVERAGE, or BELOW_AVERAGE. Explains one component of quality score.
google.ad_relevanceSTRINGAd relevance rating: ABOVE_AVERAGE, AVERAGE, or BELOW_AVERAGE. Explains one component of quality score.
google.landing_page_experienceSTRINGLanding page experience rating: ABOVE_AVERAGE, AVERAGE, or BELOW_AVERAGE. Explains one component of quality score.
google.first_page_cpcFLOAT64Estimated CPC needed to show ads on the first page of search results.
google.top_of_page_cpcFLOAT64Estimated CPC needed to show ads at the top of the first page.
google.search_impression_shareFLOAT64Fraction of eligible search impressions received (0.0-1.0).
microsoft.quality_scoreINT64Quality score snapshot (1-10).
microsoft.search_impression_shareFLOAT64Fraction of eligible search impressions received (0.0-1.0).
microsoft.impression_lost_to_budget_pctFLOAT64Estimated fraction of impressions lost due to insufficient budget (0.0-1.0).
microsoft.impression_lost_to_rank_pctFLOAT64Estimated fraction of impressions lost due to ad rank (0.0-1.0).
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline wrote this row.

⬡ads_creatives

Individual ads and creatives across all platforms. Creative content stored as schemaless JSON (creative_content) — processors dump everything the platform API returns. ad_type is deterministic from the platform and tells you what shape to expect. Intelligence STRUCT provides AI-classified content type, message theme, performance rating, and fatigue detection. Source: pulled daily from platform APIs.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, platform, ad_group_id
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, linkedin, microsoft, tiktok.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID. FK → ads_accounts.platform_account_id.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
campaign_idSTRING NOT NULLParent campaign ID. FK → ads_campaigns.campaign_id.
ad_group_idSTRING NOT NULLParent ad group ID. FK → ads_ad_groups.ad_group_id.
creative_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad or creative ID. Google: Ad ID. Meta: Ad ID. LinkedIn: Creative URN. Unique within (platform, account_id).
creative_nameSTRINGAd/creative display name as set in the platform.
platform_entity_typeSTRINGWhat the platform calls this entity. "Ad" for Google/Meta/Microsoft/TikTok, "Creative" for LinkedIn.
statusSTRINGCreative status: ENABLED, PAUSED, REMOVED, DISAPPROVED, etc. Normalized across platforms.
ad_typeSTRINGAd format type — deterministic from the platform API. Google: RESPONSIVE_SEARCH_AD, RESPONSIVE_DISPLAY_AD, VIDEO_AD, etc. Meta: VIDEO, PHOTO, CAROUSEL, COLLECTION. LinkedIn: SINGLE_IMAGE, VIDEO, CAROUSEL, DOCUMENT. Microsoft: ResponsiveSearchAd, ExpandedTextAd. TikTok: SINGLE_VIDEO, SINGLE_IMAGE, CAROUSEL, PLAYABLE.
creative_contentJSONAll creative content from the platform API as-is. Schemaless — shape varies by platform and ad_type. Query with JSON_VALUE(creative_content, "$.field_name").
health.days_runningINT64Days since first impression date from ads_creative_performance.
health.is_activeBOOLTRUE if the creative has impressions in the last 7 days.
health.ctr_trendSTRINGClick-through rate trend: improving, stable, degrading. Compares 7-day average to prior 7-day average.
health.ctr_declining_weeksINT64Consecutive weeks where CTR declined week-over-week. >= 3 is a strong fatigue signal. Feeds intelligence.fatigue_signal.
health.computed_atTIMESTAMPWhen health was last computed.
intelligence.message_themeSTRINGAI-assessed message theme: product_demo, testimonial, discount_offer, educational, brand_story, comparison, urgency, social_proof.
intelligence.performanceSTRINGAI-assessed performance rating: top_performer, above_average, average, below_average, fatiguing.
intelligence.fatigue_signalBOOLTRUE when CTR is declining over time, indicating creative fatigue. Computed from ads_creative_performance trends.
intelligence.summarySTRINGAI-generated plain-English summary of the creative content and performance within its campaign context.
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64AI confidence in the intelligence assessment (0.0-1.0).
intelligence.last_analyzed_atTIMESTAMPWhen the AI last analyzed this creative.
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen the ad/creative was created in the ad platform.
updated_atTIMESTAMPWhen the ad/creative was last modified in the ad platform.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.

⬡ads_creative_performance

Weekly performance snapshots per ad creative. Aggregates 7 days of metrics for trend analysis and fatigue detection. Normalized core plus platform-specific STRUCTs with native metrics (Google RSA asset performance, Meta reach/frequency, LinkedIn engagement, TikTok video completion). Source: aggregated weekly from platform ad-level reports.

Partition: dateCluster: platform, account_id, campaign_id
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, microsoft, linkedin, tiktok.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID. FK → ads_accounts.platform_account_id.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID that owns this ad account.
campaign_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform campaign ID. FK -> ads_campaigns.campaign_id.
ad_group_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad group ID. FK -> ads_ad_groups.ad_group_id.
creative_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad/creative ID. FK -> ads_creatives.creative_id.
dateDATE NOT NULLDate of the performance metrics. Partition key. Roll up to weekly with DATE_TRUNC(date, WEEK).
impressionsINT64Number of times the creative was shown during the snapshot week.
destination_clicksINT64Clicks that send users to a destination URL. Normalized across platforms: link clicks (Meta), headline clicks (LinkedIn), clicks (Google/MSFT), landing page clicks (TikTok).
spendFLOAT64Amount spent on this creative during the snapshot week, in account currency.
ctrFLOAT64Click-through rate for the snapshot week (destination_clicks / impressions).
cpcFLOAT64Cost per click for the snapshot week (spend / destination_clicks).
platform_conversionsFLOAT64Conversion count as reported by the platform. FLOAT64 because some platforms report fractional/modeled conversions.
platform_conversion_valueFLOAT64Total conversion value as reported by the platform, in account currency.
google.search_impression_shareFLOAT64Fraction of eligible impressions received (0.0-1.0). Search campaigns only.
google.top_impression_rateFLOAT64Fraction of impressions shown in top positions above organic results (0.0-1.0).
google.asset_performanceJSONPer-headline and per-description performance ratings for Responsive Search Ads. JSON object keyed by asset text.
meta.reachINT64Number of unique users who saw the creative during the snapshot week.
meta.frequencyFLOAT64Average number of times each unique user saw the creative (impressions / reach).
meta.inline_link_clicksINT64Inline link clicks as reported by Meta. May differ from destination_clicks due to attribution differences.
meta.purchasesINT64Number of purchase conversion events attributed to this creative.
meta.purchase_valueFLOAT64Total value of purchase conversions in account currency.
meta.video_playsINT64Total video plays/starts.
meta.video_p25INT64Video plays that reached 25% completion.
meta.video_p50INT64Video plays that reached 50% completion.
meta.video_p75INT64Video plays that reached 75% completion.
meta.video_p100INT64Video plays that reached 100% completion.
linkedin.total_engagementsINT64Total engagements (reactions + comments + shares + follows + other interactions).
linkedin.likesINT64Likes/reactions on the creative.
linkedin.commentsINT64Comments on the creative.
linkedin.sharesINT64Shares/reposts of the creative.
linkedin.followsINT64Follows generated by the creative.

⬡ads_clicks

Ad click records linking platform clicks to on-site sessions. Two sources: (1) Google click_view API with per-click GCLID and exact cost, (2) session-inferred from captured click IDs (fbclid, msclkid, li_fat_id, ttclid) in URL parameters. Source discriminator distinguishes origin. Used for closed-loop attribution.

Partition: click_dateCluster: platform, account_id, click_id
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, microsoft, linkedin, tiktok.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID that owns this ad account.
click_idSTRING NOT NULLThe platform click identifier (gclid, fbclid, msclkid, li_fat_id, or ttclid value).
click_id_typeSTRINGType of click identifier: gclid (Google), fbclid (Meta), msclkid (Microsoft), li_fat_id (LinkedIn), ttclid (TikTok).
click_dateDATE NOT NULLDate the click occurred. Partition key.
campaign_idSTRINGPlatform campaign ID. From API (Google) or from session ll_campaign_id (others). Key JOIN field to ads_metrics_daily for spend blending.
campaign_nameSTRINGCampaign name. From API (Google) or from session ll_campaign (others). NULL if platform does not insert names in URLs.
ad_group_idSTRINGPlatform ad group / ad set ID. From API (Google) or from session ll_adset_id (others).
ad_group_nameSTRINGAd group / ad set name. From session ll_adset (others). NULL if not available.
ad_idSTRINGPlatform ad/creative ID. From API (Google) or from session ll_ad_id (others).
ad_nameSTRINGAd/creative name. From session ll_ad (others). NULL if not available.
keyword_idSTRINGPlatform keyword ID that triggered this click. Google and Microsoft search campaigns only. NULL for other platforms.
keyword_textSTRINGKeyword text that triggered this click. NULL for non-search campaigns.
match_typeSTRINGKeyword match type: exact, phrase, broad. NULL for non-search campaigns.
cost_microsINT64Per-click cost in micros (millionths of the account currency). Google Ads only — other platforms do not report per-click cost. NULL for non-Google platforms.
deviceSTRINGDevice type of the click: desktop, mobile, tablet. NULL if not reported.
networkSTRINGAd network where the click occurred. Google/MSFT: g (search), s (search partner), d (display), a (audience). TikTok: tiktok, pangle. NULL for Meta and LinkedIn.
click_timestampTIMESTAMPWhen the click occurred. For platform_api source: from the API. For session_inferred: session start time (closest proxy to actual click time).
session_idSTRINGOur session ID that consumed this click. For session_inferred: always populated. For platform_api: populated after matching click_id to a session. NULL if unmatched.
device_idSTRINGDevice ID from the matched session. Used for person/company resolution downstream. NULL if no session matched.
sourceSTRINGHow this click record was created: platform_api (synced from ad platform API) or session_inferred (click ID extracted from URL parameters in our session data).
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline wrote this row.

⬡ads_conversion_uploads

Audit trail of conversions sent BACK to ad platforms for bidding optimization. When our system detects a conversion with a click ID, we upload to the originating platform. Logs: conversion details, matched click ID, platform, upload status, response. Source: our conversion upload processor.

Partition: upload_dateCluster: platform, account_id
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, microsoft, linkedin, tiktok.
platform_account_idSTRING NOT NULLPlatform ad account ID.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID that owns this ad account.
upload_idSTRING NOT NULLOur unique ID for this upload record.
upload_dateDATE NOT NULLDate the upload was attempted. Partition key.
conversion_idSTRINGOur internal conversion ID linking to our conversion tracking data.
conversion_actionSTRINGPlatform conversion goal/action name (e.g., Purchase, Lead, SignUp). Maps to the configured conversion action in the ad platform.
click_idSTRINGClick identifier matched to this conversion (gclid, fbclid, msclkid, li_fat_id, or ttclid value). NULL for enhanced conversions that match on PII only.
click_id_typeSTRINGType of click identifier: gclid (Google), fbclid (Meta), msclkid (Microsoft), li_fat_id (LinkedIn), ttclid (TikTok). NULL when no click ID was matched.
conversion_timestampTIMESTAMPWhen the conversion originally occurred (not when we uploaded it).
conversion_valueFLOAT64Monetary value of the conversion in the currency specified by currency_code.
currency_codeSTRINGISO 4217 currency code for the conversion value (e.g., USD, EUR, GBP).
user_identifiers_sentJSONWhich PII hashes were included in the upload as a JSON object. Keys are identifier types (email_sha256, phone_sha256, first_name_sha256, last_name_sha256, address_sha256). Values are booleans indicating presence. Does NOT contain actual PII or hashes.
upload_statusSTRINGCurrent status of the upload: success (platform accepted), failed (our upload call failed), pending (queued but not yet sent), rejected (platform returned an error for this conversion).
upload_responseJSONRaw platform API response for this upload. Contains error details for failed/rejected uploads. NULL for pending uploads.
uploaded_atTIMESTAMPWhen we sent this conversion to the platform API. NULL for pending uploads.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline wrote this row.

⬡ads_conversion_actions

Conversion goal definitions configured in each ad platform. One row per action (Purchase, Lead, Sign Up, etc.). Contains config: category, counting type, attribution model, windows. Intelligence STRUCT: AI-assessed business importance, mapping to our events, duplicate and misconfiguration detection. Source: platform conversion/goal APIs on connect and weekly.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, platform
FieldTypeDescription
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, microsoft, linkedin, tiktok.

account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID that owns this ad account.

action_nameSTRINGDisplay name of the conversion action as configured in the platform.
categorySTRINGConversion category: purchase, lead, signup, pageview, phone_call, app_install, custom. Platform-specific values normalized where possible.
counting_typeSTRINGHow conversions are counted: one_per_click, many_per_click (Google), unique, all (Meta). Affects whether repeated conversions from the same click are counted.
attribution_modelSTRINGAttribution model: data_driven, last_click, first_click, linear, time_decay, position_based. Platform-specific values.
click_through_window_daysINT64Number of days after a click during which a conversion is attributed to the ad (e.g., 30).
view_through_window_daysINT64Number of days after a view (impression) during which a conversion is attributed to the ad (e.g., 1).

statusSTRINGConversion action status: enabled, removed, hidden. Normalized across platforms.
value_typeSTRINGHow conversion value is determined: use_default, use_dynamic, none.
default_valueFLOAT64Default conversion value in account currency. Used when value_type is use_default.
currency_codeSTRINGISO 4217 currency code for the default_value (e.g., USD, EUR).
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen the conversion action was created in the ad platform.
intelligence.business_importanceSTRINGAI-assessed importance: critical (revenue), high (qualified lead), medium (engagement), low (pageview), noise (misconfigured).
intelligence.maps_to_ll_eventSTRINGWhich ListenLayer conversion event type this corresponds to, if any. NULL if no mapping found.
intelligence.is_revenue_actionBOOLTRUE if this represents actual revenue (purchase, subscription).
intelligence.is_lead_actionBOOLTRUE if this represents lead generation.
intelligence.duplicate_ofSTRINGAction ID of another conversion action this duplicates, if AI detects redundancy. NULL if not a duplicate.
intelligence.misconfigured_reasonSTRINGExplanation of detected misconfiguration (e.g., counting_type wrong, window too long). NULL if configuration looks correct.
intelligence.summarySTRINGAI-generated plain-English summary of the conversion action, its importance, and any issues.
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64AI confidence in the intelligence assessment (0.0-1.0).

⬡ads_budget_summary

Monthly budget tracking per ad account and cross-platform. One row per platform account per month, plus an "all" rollup row. target_monthly_budget is AI-inferred from campaign budgets or user-overridden. Pacing STRUCT tracks spend vs target with projections. Intelligence STRUCT provides budget health assessment and recommendations. Updated daily.

Partition: monthCluster: account_id, platform
FieldTypeDescription
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID.
platformSTRING NOT NULLAd platform: google, meta, linkedin, microsoft, tiktok, or "all" for the cross-platform rollup row.
platform_account_idSTRINGPlatform ad account ID. NULL for the cross-platform rollup row (platform = "all").
monthDATE NOT NULLFirst day of the month this row covers (e.g., 2026-05-01). Partition key.
currency_codeSTRINGCurrency for all monetary values in this row. For cross-platform rollup (platform = "all"): the common currency if all accounts share one, or NULL if accounts use different currencies.
target_monthly_budgetFLOAT64Monthly budget target. AI-inferred from campaign budgets (daily campaigns: daily_budget * days_in_month, lifetime campaigns: prorated portion for this month) or user-overridden.
budget_sourceSTRINGHow target_monthly_budget was determined: inferred (AI calculated from campaign budgets), user_override (user set this value manually).
month_spendFLOAT64Total spend this month to date. Sum of ads_metrics_daily.spend for this platform account and month.
month_impressionsINT64Total impressions this month to date.
month_clicksINT64Total destination clicks this month to date.
month_conversionsFLOAT64Total platform conversions this month to date.
days_with_dataINT64Number of days in this month that have spend data. Used to compute averages.
active_campaignsINT64Number of campaigns with spend in the last 7 days.
paused_campaignsINT64Number of campaigns in PAUSED status.
campaigns_underpacingINT64Number of active campaigns where pacing.pacing_status = underpacing.
campaigns_overpacingINT64Number of active campaigns where pacing.pacing_status = overpacing.
campaigns_exhaustedINT64Number of campaigns where pacing.pacing_status = exhausted.
pacing.pacing_ratioFLOAT64month_spend / target_monthly_budget. 0.50 on the 15th = on track. 0.50 on the 25th = underpacing. NULL if no target budget.
pacing.expected_ratioFLOAT64Where pacing_ratio should be today: day_of_month / days_in_month. Compare to pacing_ratio to see if ahead or behind.
pacing.pacing_statusSTRINGon_track, underpacing, overpacing, exhausted, no_budget. Derived from pacing_ratio vs expected_ratio.
pacing.avg_daily_spendFLOAT64month_spend / days_with_data. Actual average daily spend this month.
pacing.target_daily_spendFLOAT64(target_monthly_budget - month_spend) / days_remaining. What needs to be spent per day to hit the target.
pacing.projected_month_spendFLOAT64avg_daily_spend * days_in_month. Where we will end up if current rate continues.
pacing.projected_varianceFLOAT64projected_month_spend - target_monthly_budget. Positive = projected overspend. Negative = projected underspend.
pacing.computed_atTIMESTAMPWhen pacing was last computed.
intelligence.budget_healthSTRINGAI-assessed overall budget health: healthy, attention_needed, at_risk, critical.
intelligence.top_issueSTRINGThe single most important budget issue right now. NULL if no issues.
intelligence.recommendationsJSONJSON array of actionable recommendations. NULL if no recommendations.
intelligence.summarySTRINGAI-generated plain-English summary of budget health across all campaigns for this platform (or all platforms for the rollup row).
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64AI confidence in the assessment (0.0-1.0).
intelligence.last_analyzed_atTIMESTAMPWhen the AI last analyzed this budget summary.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.

⬡web_events

Every web event captured by the browser SDK — pageviews, clicks, scrolls, form interactions, ecommerce actions, identity detections, consent changes. One row per event with 24+ typed STRUCTs containing full event detail (page, device, geo, bot assessment, attribution, form, ecommerce, person, company, triggers, conversions). Source: browser SDK → Cloudflare Edge → collect-processor. Used for: all web analytics, session building, identity resolution, trigger evaluation.

⬡web_events_raw

Raw web event payloads — archive of every event before processing. Contains the full wire-format JSON as received from the collect gateway. Source: collect-processor archives every inbound event. Used for: debugging, reprocessing, schema evolution (new fields captured without DDL changes). NOT shared with customers.

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: account_id, event_type, device_id
FieldTypeDescription
event_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique event identifier
event_typeSTRING NOT NULLWire short code (pv, clk, pp, pc, etc.) — NOT expanded to human-readable names
event_sourceSTRINGEvent source: web, app, or phone. From base.esrc or defaulted by collector
timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLEvent timestamp
account_idSTRINGAccount identifier
device_idSTRINGDevice identifier
parent_event_idSTRINGParent event ID for child events
edge_generatedBOOLWhether the event was generated by the edge worker
bot_scoreINT64Cloudflare bot score (0-100, higher = more human)
bot_verifiedBOOLCloudflare verified bot flag
payloadJSONFull wire event from edge — the complete JSON payload. Phone data in payload.phn, triggers in payload.trg, conversions in payload.wconv
received_atTIMESTAMPWhen the event was received by the collector
processed_atTIMESTAMPWhen the event was processed into web_events
removed_from_web_events_atTIMESTAMPWhen the typed web_events row was intentionally removed (e.g. no-pageview session cleanup)
removed_from_web_events_reasonSTRINGReason for removal from web_events, e.g. no_pageview_session

⬡events_clicks

Every click (web) and tap (app) — unified breakout table. One row per interaction with element details, destination URLs, and interaction type. Web and app rows share the same table; event_source discriminates. Source: raw_to_web_events transform. Used for: interaction analytics, outbound click tracking, button performance analysis.

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: account_id, event_source, device_id
FieldTypeDescription
event_sourceSTRING NOT NULLSource pipeline: web or app
source_idSTRINGWhich specific source instance: web=hostname, app=bundle_id
event_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique event identifier
session_idSTRINGSession this click/tap belongs to. FK to sessions table
device_idSTRINGAnonymous device identifier. FK to person_devices for identity resolution
pageview_idSTRINGView this click/tap occurred on. Web: pageview_id. App: screen_view_id
timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen the click/tap event occurred (UTC)
account_idSTRINGListenLayer account ID that owns this data
interaction_idSTRINGUnified click/tap identifier. Same as event_id for web. Enables cross-source JOIN without CASE statement
interaction_labelSTRINGWhat was clicked/tapped. Web: COALESCE(text, destination_url). App: COALESCE(text, accessibility_label, component_name). GROUP BY this for unified cross-source rankings
interaction_target_pathSTRINGWhere the click/tap navigated. Web: destination_url. App: deep link target. NULL if no navigation occurred
click_typeSTRINGClick classification: link, button, image, etc. Web only. NULL for app rows
tag_nameSTRINGHTML tag of the clicked element: A, BUTTON, DIV, IMG, etc. Web only. NULL for app rows
textSTRINGVisible text content of the clicked element (truncated). Web only. NULL for app rows
destination_urlSTRINGFull destination URL for link clicks. Web only. NULL for app rows
is_externalBOOLTRUE when the click destination is a different domain. Web only. NULL for app rows
downloadSTRINGFile extension or name when the click is a file download (e.g. pdf, xlsx). Web only. NULL for app rows
ruleSTRINGClick tracking rule that matched this click. Web only. NULL for app rows
css_classesARRAY<STRING>CSS classes on the clicked element. Web only. NULL for app rows
image_srcSTRINGImage source URL when an image was clicked. Web only. NULL for app rows
page_pathSTRINGURL path of the page where the click occurred. Web only. NULL for app rows
page_hostnameSTRINGHostname of the page where the click occurred. Web only. NULL for app rows
processed_atTIMESTAMPWhen this row was written by the transform pipeline
app_context.screen_nameSTRINGScreen where the tap occurred. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.screen_classSTRINGController/view class name for the screen. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.app_bundle_idSTRINGApp bundle identifier, e.g. com.acme.consumer. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.app_nameSTRINGDisplay name of the app. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.platformSTRINGios or android. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.app_versionSTRINGSemantic app version, e.g. 4.2.1. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.sdk_versionSTRINGListenLayer mobile SDK version. App only. NULL for web rows

app_tap_target.component_classSTRINGComponent class name, e.g. UIButton, android.widget.Button, TouchableOpacity. App only. NULL for web rows
app_tap_target.accessibility_labelSTRINGAccessibility label of the tapped element, e.g. Buy Now, Add to Cart. App only. NULL for web rows
app_tap_target.accessibility_idSTRINGDeveloper-set test/automation ID on the element. App only. NULL for web rows
app_tap_target.textSTRINGVisible text on the tapped button or element. App only. NULL for web rows

app_tap_target.tap_yFLOAT64Y coordinate of the tap within the screen in logical pixels. App only. NULL for web rows

⬡events_triggers

Trigger fire log — one row per trigger rule match across web, app, CRM, phone. Records which trigger fired on which event, whether it was a conversion. Source: collect-processor + CRM ingest processor. Used for: conversion tracking, trigger debugging, rule performance analysis.

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: account_id, trigger_id, is_conversion
FieldTypeDescription
event_sourceSTRING NOT NULLSource pipeline: web, app, crm, phone
source_idSTRINGWhich specific source instance: web=hostname, app=bundle_id, crm=org_id, phone=phone.
source_org_idSTRINGMulti-tenant disambiguation: SF org ID, ad account ID, etc. NULL for web/app.
event_idSTRING NOT NULLOriginating event ID. References the typed table for the source (web_events, app_events, crm_changelog, phone_events).
session_idSTRINGSession where the trigger fired. NULL for CRM and phone-static rows.
device_idSTRINGDevice that triggered. NULL for CRM and phone-static rows.
timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen the fire-log row was written.
event_timestampTIMESTAMPMeaningful business time. Web/app: equals timestamp. CRM: from registry config (e.g., Opportunity.CloseDate). Phone: call significant timestamp.
account_idSTRINGListenLayer account ID that owns this data.
trigger_idSTRINGFK to triggers_config. The rule that matched.
slugSTRINGHuman-readable rule slug.
is_conversionBOOLConvenience flag: TRUE if this trigger is a conversion trigger.
event_typeSTRINGWeb/app: SDK event type (pageview, form_submit, etc.). CRM: rule type (crm_field_change, crm_field_value). Phone: call event subtype.
page_pathSTRINGURL path of the page where the trigger fired.
page_hostnameSTRINGHostname of the page where the trigger fired.
crm_object_typeSTRINGSF object: Lead, Contact, Opportunity, etc. Top-level for direct filtering.
crm_record_idSTRINGSF record ID. Top-level for direct filtering and joining.
crm_context.change_typeSTRINGcreate, update, delete, undelete
crm_context.field_nameSTRINGWhich field changed (NULL for create/delete)
crm_context.old_valueSTRINGPrevious field value before the change.
crm_context.new_valueSTRINGNew field value after the change.
crm_context.actor_user_idSTRINGSF user who made the change
crm_context.modstampTIMESTAMPSystemModstamp (distinct from event_timestamp)
app_context.screen_nameSTRINGScreen where the trigger fired.
app_context.screen_classSTRINGController/view class name for the screen.
app_context.app_bundle_idSTRINGcom.acme.consumer — the hostname equivalent
app_context.app_nameSTRINGDisplay name of the app.
app_context.platformSTRINGios or android
app_context.app_versionSTRINGSemantic app version (e.g., 4.2.1).
app_context.sdk_versionSTRINGListenLayer mobile SDK version.
is_backfilledBOOLTRUE for rows produced by historical backfill (Plan 69). FALSE for live fires.
processed_atTIMESTAMPWhen this row was written by the transform pipeline.

⬡events_conversions

Conversion event log — one row per conversion recorded across all sources. The definitive record of every conversion with trigger reference, value, and source context (web, CRM, phone, ads). Source: collect-processor + CRM ingest processor. Used for: conversion reporting, ROAS numerator, attribution analysis.

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: account_id, type, conversion_id

⬡app_events

Every mobile app event captured by the app SDK — screen views, taps, scrolls, app lifecycle (install, open, background, foreground), push notifications, deep links, forms, ecommerce, identity. One row per event with app-specific STRUCTs (screen_view, tap, lifecycle, push, deep_link) plus shared STRUCTs mirroring web_events (form, ecommerce, chat, video, person, company, triggers, conversions). Source: mobile SDK → collect-processor. Used for: app analytics, cross-platform session building.

⬡app_events_raw

Raw mobile app event payloads — archive of every app event before processing. Contains the full wire-format JSON as received from the app SDK. Source: collect-processor archives every inbound app event. Used for: debugging, reprocessing, schema evolution (new SDK fields captured without DDL changes). NOT shared with customers.

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: account_id, event_type, device_id
FieldTypeDescription
event_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique event identifier
event_typeSTRING NOT NULLWire short codes — NOT expanded to human-readable names. Values: sv (screen_view), tap, scr (scroll), ss (session_state), ai (app_install), ao (app_open), ab (app_background), af (app_foreground), au (app_update), dl (deep_link_open), pr (push_received), po (push_opened), fl (form_load), fv (form_visible), fs (form_start), fsu (form_submit), pp (person detection), id (identify), ic (identify company), cus (custom), evi (ecommerce view_item), epu (ecommerce purchase), atc (add_to_cart), rfc (refund), etc.
event_sourceSTRINGEvent source: web, app, or phone. From base.esrc in the wire payload
timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this event occurred (UTC)
account_idSTRINGCustomer account ID
device_idSTRINGApp install identifier: IDFV (iOS), AAID (Android), or SDK-generated UUID
app_bundle_idSTRINGApp bundle identifier, e.g. com.acme.consumer. Equivalent to hostname for web events
platformSTRINGApp platform: ios, android, react_native, flutter
parent_event_idSTRINGEvent ID of the parent event that triggered this one
sdk_generatedBOOLTRUE if the SDK generated this event internally, e.g. session_state heartbeats. Equivalent to edge_generated on web
payloadJSONFull wire event JSON from the app SDK. Source of truth for reprocessing. Phone assignment data in payload.phn array
received_atTIMESTAMPWhen our system received this event
processed_atTIMESTAMPWhen the event was processed into app_events. Set by the pipeline orchestrator mark_processed step

⬡app_screens

Catalog of every unique app screen observed. One row per unique screen per app. Contains AI-generated topic classification and screen type. Screens are discovered from screen_view events — not crawler-fed like web_pages. Paired with app_screen_versions for classification history. Source: classification-processor on first observation and reclassification. Used for: app content analytics, screen-level reporting, AI-driven screen categorization.

Partition: none (no partitioning — small catalog, one row per unique screen per app)Cluster: app_bundle_id, screen_name
FieldTypeDescription
screen_idSTRING NOT NULLHash of (app_bundle_id, platform, screen_name). Primary lookup key
account_idSTRINGCustomer account ID
app_bundle_idSTRINGApp bundle identifier, e.g. com.acme.consumer
platformSTRINGApp platform: ios or android
screen_nameSTRINGDeveloper-assigned screen name, e.g. PricingScreen, HomeScreen. Primary human-readable identifier
screen_classSTRINGView controller or Activity class name, e.g. PricingViewController
screen_routeSTRINGRoute path if the app uses a router-based navigation system, e.g. /pricing
topicSTRINGAI-classified primary topic of the screen (e.g. Pricing, Onboarding, Product Detail)
sub_topicSTRINGAI-classified sub-topic of the screen
screen_typeSTRINGAI-classified screen type: content, form, list, detail, settings, checkout, auth
summarySTRINGAI-generated 1-2 sentence summary of the screen purpose
custom_classificationJSONCustomer-defined classification rules applied to this screen
classifiedBOOLTRUE when AI classification has been applied. Default FALSE
classified_atTIMESTAMPWhen AI classification was last run on this screen
classification_modelSTRINGLLM model used for the current classification, e.g. grok-4-fast
prunedBOOLTRUE if this screen has been pruned from the active catalog. Default FALSE
first_seenTIMESTAMPWhen this screen was first observed via a screen_view event
last_seenTIMESTAMPWhen this screen was most recently observed
view_countINT64Total screen views across all devices for this screen
updated_atTIMESTAMPWhen this row was last updated

⬡app_screen_versions

History of AI classification changes for app screens. One row per classification update per screen. A new row is written each time the AI reclassifies a screen (e.g. when the classification prompt version changes). Paired with app_screens which holds the current state. Source: classification-processor on reclassification. Used for: classification audit trail, tracking how screen purpose classification evolved over time.

Partition: noneCluster: screen_id
FieldTypeDescription
screen_idSTRING NOT NULLFK to app_screens.screen_id. Identifies which screen this version belongs to
version_idSTRING NOT NULLUUID uniquely identifying this classification version
account_idSTRINGCustomer account ID
app_bundle_idSTRINGApp bundle identifier, e.g. com.acme.consumer. Denormalized for query convenience
screen_nameSTRINGDeveloper screen name at the time of this classification, e.g. PricingScreen
screen_classSTRINGView controller or Activity class name at the time of this classification
topicSTRINGAI-classified primary topic at this version
sub_topicSTRINGAI-classified sub-topic at this version
screen_typeSTRINGAI-classified screen type at this version: content, form, list, detail, settings, checkout, auth
summarySTRINGAI-generated summary of the screen purpose at this version

⬡persons

Resolved person records. One row per unique identified individual across all sources (web forms, CRM contacts/leads, app SDK, ads lead gen). Contains name, email, phone, company, job title, enrichment data, engagement summary, AI intelligence. The central identity table. Source: identity-resolver processor from persons_raw. Used for: people-level analytics, identity resolution, CRM-to-web matching.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, active, email_hash
FieldTypeDescription

email_hash_md5STRINGMD5 hash of the normalized primary email address. Used for legacy integrations.
phone_hashSTRINGSHA-256 hash of the normalized primary phone number.
phone_hash_md5STRINGMD5 hash of the normalized primary phone number. Used for legacy integrations.

person_type.is_importedBOOLPerson was imported via CSV, CRM sync, or API bulk upload.

⬡persons_raw

Raw person detection events before identity resolution. One row per detection from any source (web form, CRM import, ads lead gen, app SDK). Multiple rows may represent the same real person. Source: collect-processor, CRM ingest processor, ads processors. Used for: feeding the identity resolution pipeline that creates/updates persons.

Partition: DATE(created_at)Cluster: account_id, email_hash, resolution_status
FieldTypeDescription

account_idSTRINGListenLayer account ID that owns this data.
email_hashSTRINGSHA-256 of email — primary dedup key, nullable for phone-only persons
email_hash_md5STRINGMD5 hash of email for ad platform matching
phone_hashSTRINGSHA-256 of E.164 phone — fallback dedup key when no email
phone_hash_md5STRINGMD5 of E.164 phone for ad platform matching
emailSTRINGPlaintext email from decryption
phoneSTRINGPlaintext phone from decryption — E.164 when validated
sourceSTRINGDetection source: detected, explicit, reveal, crm_lead, crm_contact (CRM via esrc=crm), ads_lead_gen (LinkedIn/Google/Meta lead gen forms via esrc=ads)
detection_typeSTRINGDetection method: form, network, datalayer, url_param, chat, scheduling, ecommerce, video, custom
confidenceFLOAT64Detection confidence score 0-1
likely_submitterBOOLEANPrimary person from multi-person form submission
email_deliverabilitySTRINGEmail deliverability status: DELIVERABLE, UNDELIVERABLE, RISKY — gates enrichment spend
device_idSTRINGDevice that generated this detection
source_event_idSTRINGThe pp/id event ID that triggered this detection
parent_event_idSTRINGParent event for person-company cross-reference
bot_scoreINT64Bot likelihood score 0-100, used for enrichment gating
bot_verifiedBOOLEANWhether this is a known good bot
person_dataJSONComplete wire event person block as JSON — resolution processor reads this for all fields; new edge fields captured without schema changes
enrichment_statusSTRINGEnrichment pipeline status: pending, enriched, partial, failed, skipped
enriched_atTIMESTAMPTimestamp when enrichment completed
enrichment_sourceSTRINGEnrichment provider identifier
enrichment_dataJSONRaw enrichment API responses as JSON

resolved_atTIMESTAMPTimestamp when resolution completed

last_seenTIMESTAMP NOT NULLMost recent observation of this person detection
occurrence_countINT64Number of times this person detection has been observed
created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLRow creation timestamp
updated_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLRow last-updated timestamp

⬡person_devices

Person-to-device associations. One row per (person, device) pair. Links anonymous device activity to identified people. Source: identity-resolver processor when person detection occurs on a device. Used for: connecting anonymous sessions to known people, multi-device attribution.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, device_id, person_id
FieldTypeDescription
person_idSTRING NOT NULLFK to persons table (person_id) — set by Op 1 enrichment pipeline
device_idSTRING NOT NULLDevice ID from the wire event (base.did) — set by Op 1
account_idSTRINGListenLayer account ID that owns this data.
sourceSTRING NOT NULLHow this link was established: pp_event, id_event, merge_inherited, llm_resolution, import, crm_identity (CRM-sourced from Plan 69)
source_event_idSTRINGThe event ID (base.evtid) that created this link
confidenceFLOAT64Confidence in this link: 1.0 for explicit identify, 0.8 for form pp, lower for inferred
is_primaryBOOLEANDEPRECATED — use ownership_status instead
ownership_statusSTRINGDevice ownership: primary (device owner), secondary (legitimate but not owner), displaced (was primary, bumped by stronger signal). NULL = not yet evaluated. Set by device ownership resolver (Rules 1-5)
detection_countINT64Number of independent events linking this person to this device. Incremented on repeat detections and dedup merges. Higher count = stronger ownership signal
activeBOOLEANFALSE if link was disputed, revoked, or stale
first_seenTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this person-device link was first established
last_seenTIMESTAMP NOT NULLMost recent event confirming this link
_cascade_checkedBOOLEANTRUE after the relationship scanner has processed this row for cascade to person_companies
_created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLRow creation timestamp
_updated_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLRow last-updated timestamp

⬡person_companies

Person-to-company associations. One row per (person, company) pair with confidence and relationship metadata. Source: identity-resolver processor from shared device/domain signals. Used for: connecting people to companies, account-based analytics.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, person_id, company_domain
FieldTypeDescription

source_event_idSTRINGThe event that established this link (if event-based). NULL for scanner-created or enrichment-created links.
source_confidenceSTRINGhigh \medium \low. Strength of this signal in isolation.
confidence_reasonSTRINGShort identifier explaining why this row got its source_confidence (e.g. llm_resolved_current_employer, device_proximity_only).
confidenceFLOAT64DEPRECATED. Legacy float, populated from source_confidence via fixed map (high=0.9, medium=0.6, low=0.3). Will be dropped once nothing reads it.
titleSTRINGJob title at this company, if known from enrichment employment_history
is_currentBOOLEANWhether this is a current relationship. Set TRUE on creation, may be set FALSE by Op 4 when employment_history shows role ended.
activeBOOLEANFALSE if relationship deactivated (stale, disputed, cleaned up by Op 4)

last_seenTIMESTAMP NOT NULLMost recent event or scan confirming this relationship

Person activity changelog. One row per significant change — creation, merge, enrichment update, field change. Audit trail of how person data evolved. Source: identity-resolver processor. Used for: identity audit trail, merge history, enrichment tracking.

Partition: DATE(created_at)Cluster: account_id, person_id

previous_valuesSTRINGJSON of prior values before this change, e.g. {"email": null, "first_name": null}

⬡_person_match_decisions

Internal cache of LLM-evaluated person deduplication comparisons. One row per evaluated person pair. Prevents re-evaluating the same pair on every dedup scan by caching the decision result. Mirrors the same pattern as _company_match_decisions. Source: identity-resolver dedup scanner. Used for: dedup performance optimization — the scanner checks this table before spending LLM tokens re-evaluating a pair it has already assessed.

Partition: noneCluster: none
FieldTypeDescription
person_id_aSTRING NOT NULLFirst person ID in the pair. Always the lexicographically smaller UUID (person_id_a < person_id_b) to ensure each pair has exactly one canonical row regardless of evaluation order
account_idSTRINGListenLayer account ID that owns this data
person_id_bSTRING NOT NULLSecond person ID in the pair. Always the lexicographically larger UUID (person_id_a < person_id_b)
decisionSTRING NOT NULLMatch decision result: same_person (records represent the same individual and should be merged), different_person (records are distinct individuals), or uncertain (insufficient signal to decide)
confidenceFLOAT64Confidence score for the decision: 0.0 to 1.0. Higher values indicate stronger certainty in the decision
reasoningSTRINGLLM-generated plain-English explanation of why this decision was made, including the signals considered
decided_bySTRING NOT NULLWho or what made the decision: auto_merge (rule-based automatic merge), llm_auto (LLM-evaluated without human review), or manual (human reviewer decision)
decided_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this decision was recorded

is_competitorBOOLTRUE if this company is a competitor of the client. Set by competitor classification (LLM or known_competitor_domains config).
is_competitor_atTIMESTAMPPlan 76 — when is_competitor was last classified. Re-classified every 90 days so the competitive landscape does not go stale.
_created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLRow creation timestamp in BigQuery.
_reprocessingJSONPlan 60l — reprocessing state blob. Keys: needs_reprocessing, failed_steps, reasons, skipped_reason, retry_count, max_retries, first_failed_at, last_retry_at, next_retry_after, processing_version. NULL means fully processed. Same structure as persons._reprocessing.

⬡companies_raw

⬡company_devices

Company-to-device associations. One row per (company, device) pair. Links device activity to companies (via IP reveal or person-company relationships). Source: identity-resolver processor. Used for: company-level session attribution, account engagement tracking.

Internal cache of LLM-evaluated company deduplication comparisons. Prevents re-evaluating the same company pair on every dedup scan. Source: identity-resolver dedup scanner. Used for: dedup performance optimization.

⬡sessions

Session-level aggregates across web and app. One row per device per calendar day. Summarizes all activity: duration, engagement score, page/screen views, entry/exit points, attribution (UTMs, click IDs, ll_* params), triggers, conversions, forms, ecommerce, consent. Source: daily pipeline orchestrator from web_events/app_events. Used for: session-level reporting, attribution analysis, engagement scoring.

Partition: session_dateCluster: account_id, device_id, event_source
FieldTypeDescription
event_sourceSTRING NOT NULLSource pipeline: web, app
source_idSTRINGWhich specific source instance: web=hostname, app=bundle_id
session_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique session identifier
device_idSTRING NOT NULLDevice that owns this session
session_dateDATE NOT NULLCalendar date of this session. Partition key
session_numberINT64Sequential visit number for this device. 1 = first ever session
account_idSTRINGAccount UUID that owns this session data
entry_view_labelSTRINGUnified entry point. Web: entry_page.path. App: entry_screen.screen_name. GROUP BY this for cross-source entry rankings
exit_view_labelSTRINGUnified exit point. Web: exit_page.path. App: exit_screen.screen_name
parent_session_idSTRINGIf this session is a continuation, the session_id of the original session before midnight split
is_continuationBOOLTRUE if this session is a continuation of a previous session split at midnight
is_active_sessionBOOLTRUE if session met active engagement threshold (not a bounce, had meaningful interaction)
is_bounceBOOLTRUE if only one pageview/screen-view with no engagement
is_first_sessionBOOLTRUE if this is the very first session for this device (session_number = 1)
new_deviceBOOLTRUE if this session created the device row (device did not exist before this session)

llm_classification.visitor_roleSTRINGBusiness role of the visitor (executive, developer, marketer, etc.)
bot.score_avgFLOAT64Mean of bot.score across events in this session
bot.score_p10INT6410th percentile of bot.score across session events
bot.score_p50INT64Median bot.score across session events
bot.score_p90INT6490th percentile of bot.score across session events
bot.sample_countINT64Number of events in this session with non-null bot.score
bot.visitor_type_majoritySTRINGMode of bot.visitor_type across this session's events: human, agent, crawler, unknown
bot.agent_fractionFLOAT640.0-1.0 fraction of events typed as agent
bot.crawler_fractionFLOAT640.0-1.0 fraction of events typed as crawler
bot.headless_event_countINT64Count of events with bot.headless=TRUE
bot.total_event_countINT64Total events in this session (denominator for fraction fields)
bot.headless_type_firstSTRINGFirst observed headless tool when headless=TRUE (chromium, phantomjs, etc.)
bot.category_modeSTRINGMode of bot.category across events
bot.any_verifiedBOOLSession-level OR — TRUE if any event had verified=TRUE. Verified bots (Googlebot etc.) are high-trust disclosed signals
custom_propertiesJSONAccount-defined custom session properties. Schema varies by account
built_atTIMESTAMPWhen this session row was built/finalized by the transform pipeline
has_unlocked_dataBOOLTRUE if this session contains data that was unlocked by a later consent grant
_reprocessingJSONLLM reprocessing state blob. Keys: needs_reprocessing, failed_steps, reasons, skipped_reason, retry_count, max_retries, first_failed_at, last_retry_at, next_retry_after, processing_version. NULL means fully processed

⬡session_recordings

Session recording metadata and GCS pointers. One row per recorded session. Only engaged sessions are recorded — non-engaged recordings are abandoned by the SDK and never persisted. Recording data (rrweb DOM recording for web, component tree for app) is stored in GCS, not BigQuery. Events can be overlaid on the recording using started_at as the sync anchor: event_time_in_recording = web_event.timestamp - started_at. Source: collect-processor writes metadata row; recording data stored in GCS. Used for: session replay, UX analysis, debugging.

Partition: recording_dateCluster: account_id, device_id, session_id
FieldTypeDescription
recording_idSTRING NOT NULLUUID uniquely identifying this recording
account_idSTRINGAccount UUID that owns this recording
session_idSTRING NOT NULLFK to sessions table. Which session this recording captures
device_idSTRING NOT NULLFK to devices table
event_sourceSTRING NOT NULLSource of the recording: web or app
source_idSTRINGWhich specific source instance: web=hostname, app=app_bundle_id
gcs_uriSTRINGGCS path to the recording file, e.g. gs://new-ll-session-recordings-{env}/{account_id}/{recording_id}.json.gz
formatSTRINGRecording format: rrweb (web DOM recording) or component_tree (app, future)
file_size_bytesINT64Size of the GCS recording file in bytes
started_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLUTC timestamp when the recording started. The sync anchor for event overlay — event_time_in_recording = web_event.timestamp - started_at
ended_atTIMESTAMPUTC timestamp when the recording ended
duration_msINT64Total recording duration in milliseconds
event_countINT64Number of rrweb events in the recording (DOM mutations, mouse moves, scrolls, inputs)
page_countINT64Number of pages or screens visited during this recording
has_engagementBOOLTRUE if the session met the engagement threshold. Always TRUE since only engaged sessions are persisted
pii_redactedBOOLTRUE if PII masking was applied to the recording before storage
masked_selectorsJSONCSS selectors that were masked during recording, e.g. [".credit-card-input", "#ssn-field"]. NULL if no masking was applied
thumbnail_uriSTRINGGCS pointer to a screenshot thumbnail image used in the recording list UI
recording_dateDATE NOT NULLDate of the recording. Partition key
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen this metadata row was written to BigQuery

⬡view_paths

One row per pageview (web) or screen view (app) with full session and path context. Position in session, duration, scroll depth, and whether forms/conversions occurred on that view. Enables cross-session, cross-device view path analysis without UNNESTing the pages/screens arrays across many session partitions. Built from sessions.pages[] (web) or sessions.screens[] (app). Source: daily pipeline orchestrator. Used for: page path analysis, content performance, user journey visualization.

Partition: session_dateCluster: account_id, device_id, view_label
FieldTypeDescription

account_idSTRINGAccount UUID
view_idSTRING NOT NULLUnified view identifier. Web: pageview_id. App: screen_view_id
timestampTIMESTAMPWhen this view occurred
positionINT64View order within the session. 1 = first view, 2 = second, etc. Use ORDER BY position to reconstruct the path
view_labelSTRINGUnified view identifier. Web: URL path. App: screen_name. GROUP BY this for cross-source view rankings
view_typeSTRINGUnified AI classification. Web: from page_type. App: from screen classification. e.g. pricing, blog-post, checkout
hostnameSTRINGHostname of the page. Web only. NULL for app rows
pathSTRINGURL path of the page, e.g. /pricing. Web only. NULL for app rows
titleSTRINGHTML page title. Web only. NULL for app rows
page_typeSTRINGAI-classified page type. Web only. Kept for web query fluency; canonical cross-source field is view_type. NULL for app rows
topicSTRINGAI-classified topic. Permanent snapshot taken at pipeline build time — does not change if the page is reclassified later

had_conversionBOOLTRUE if a conversion trigger fired during this view
had_clickBOOLTRUE if a tracked click occurred on this view. Web only. NULL for app rows
had_tapBOOLTRUE if a tracked tap occurred on this view. App only. NULL for web rows
screen_nameSTRINGApp screen name, analog of URL path, e.g. PricingScreen. App only. NULL for web rows
screen_classSTRINGController/view class name, e.g. PricingViewController. App only. NULL for web rows
screen_routeSTRINGRoute string if the app uses a router-based navigation system. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.app_bundle_idSTRINGApp bundle identifier, e.g. com.acme.consumer. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.app_nameSTRINGDisplay name of the app. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.platformSTRINGios or android. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.app_versionSTRINGSemantic app version at the time of this view, e.g. 4.2.1. App only. NULL for web rows
app_context.sdk_versionSTRINGListenLayer mobile SDK version. App only. NULL for web rows
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When the pipeline last built this row

⬡web_pages

Catalog of every unique web page observed. One row per URL. Contains AI-generated topic classification, page type, and summary. Updated by the page crawler and classification pipeline. Source: classification-processor analyzing page content. Used for: content analytics, page-level reporting, AI-driven page categorization.

Partition: none (no partitioning — max ~10K rows per account, ~200 KB total)Cluster: page_url_hash
FieldTypeDescription

current_versionINT64 NOT NULLLatest version number. Increments each time content_hash changes
content_hashSTRING NOT NULLSHA-256 of current markdown content. Changes when page content changes
topicSTRINGOrganic broad category derived from page content by LLM (e.g. Pricing, Product Features, Case Study)
sub_topicSTRINGOrganic specific subcategory derived from page content by LLM
page_typeSTRINGStrict page type seeded from common page types list (e.g. landing, blog-post, pricing, product, contact)

sub_topic_overrideSTRINGUser-set sub_topic override. Takes precedence over LLM-generated sub_topic when set
page_type_overrideSTRINGUser-set page_type override. Takes precedence over LLM-generated page_type when set
summary_overrideSTRINGUser-set summary override. Takes precedence over LLM-generated summary when set

⬡web_page_versions

History of AI classification changes for web pages. One row per content version per URL. A new row is written whenever the page content_hash changes (i.e. the page was meaningfully updated). Tracks how a page's topic, type, and summary evolved over time. Paired with web_pages which holds the current state. Source: classification-processor on reclassification. Used for: classification audit trail, content strategy analysis.

Partition: DATE(created_at)Cluster: page_url_hash
FieldTypeDescription
page_url_hashSTRING NOT NULLFK to web_pages.page_url_hash. Identifies which page this version belongs to
account_idSTRINGListenLayer account ID that owns this data
page_urlSTRING NOT NULLDenormalized page URL for query convenience (avoids JOIN to web_pages for URL display)
versionINT64 NOT NULLMonotonic version number per URL. 1 = first version ever seen. Matches web_pages.current_version for the active version
content_hashSTRING NOT NULLSHA-256 of markdown content for this version. Each unique hash = one version row
markdownSTRINGFull page markdown content at this version (~1-5 KB). Source material for LLM classification
headingsSTRINGJSON array of h1-h6 heading text extracted from the page at this version
topicSTRINGBroad category derived from page content by LLM for this version (e.g. Pricing, Product Features, Case Study)
sub_topicSTRINGSpecific subcategory derived from page content by LLM for this version
page_typeSTRINGPage type from common page types list for this version (e.g. landing, blog-post, pricing, product, contact)
summarySTRING1-2 sentence LLM-generated summary of the page at this version
customJSONCustomer-defined key-value pairs for custom classification at this version
classification_vINT64Prompt version used for this classification. Enables detection of stale classifications when the prompt is updated
classified_atTIMESTAMPWhen this version was classified by LLM
classifier_modelSTRINGLLM model used for this classification (e.g. grok-4-fast, gemini-2.5-flash)
form_countINT64Number of forms detected on this version of the page
form_hashesARRAY<STRING>Array of form structural hashes detected on this version. Links to form_inventory table
first_seenTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen the first page content event (PCE) with this content_hash was received
last_seenTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen the last PCE with this content_hash was received (i.e. just before the next version appeared)
created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this version row was inserted into BigQuery. Partition key

⬡conversion_paths

Each path step carries attribution data, ad spend, and five pre-calculated credit weights (first-touch, last-touch, linear, position-based, time-decay). Source: daily pipeline orchestrator from touchpoints. Used for: channel attribution, campaign ROI, ROAS calculation.

⬡devices

Device lifetime records across web and app. One row per unique device (browser cookie or app install). Accumulates lifetime activity, engagement, bot assessment, first/last touch attribution, consent state, AI classification. Source: daily pipeline orchestrator from sessions. Used for: visitor-level reporting, bot detection, device-to-person resolution.

Partition: none (entity state table, not time-series)Cluster: account_id, event_source, is_bot
FieldTypeDescription
event_sourceSTRING NOT NULLSource pipeline: web or app. Set from first session and locked — never changes

account_idSTRINGAccount UUID that owns this device data
is_engagedBOOLTRUE once this device has had any session meeting engagement criteria. Permanent once set — never flips back to FALSE
is_botBOOLConvenience flag derived from bot.visitor_type. TRUE when visitor_type is agent or crawler. Overridden to FALSE when has_person flips TRUE
is_verified_botBOOLTRUE if any event identified this as a known verified bot (e.g. Googlebot). Permanent once set
has_convertedBOOLTRUE once any conversion trigger has fired for this device. Permanent once set

bot.visitor_type_confidenceSTRINGConfidence in visitor_type: high (≥80% session agreement, ≥5 sessions), medium (60-80%, ≥3 sessions), low (otherwise)

bot.score_p90INT6490th percentile of session-level score averages over the last 30 days

⬡traffic_source_rules

Traffic source classification rules. Defines how UTM parameters, referrer hosts, and click IDs map to traffic source names and channels. Source: customer configuration via dashboard. Used for: resolving session attribution into named traffic sources at query time.

Partition: noneCluster: none
FieldTypeDescription
rule_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique rule identifier (UUID).
account_idSTRINGListenLayer account ID that owns this data.
tierSTRING NOT NULLRule tier: ll, utm, click_id, referrer, catch_all.
rule_typeSTRING NOT NULLMatch type: querystring or referring (matches edge R2 table.type).
match_field_countINT64 NOT NULLNumber of non-null match fields (for specificity ranking).
priorityINT64 NOT NULLComputed priority: tier_weight*1000 + field_count*100 + row_index.
row_indexINT64Ordering within same tier+specificity (0 = first).
traffic_sourceSTRING NOT NULLAssigned traffic source label, e.g. Google Ads — Paid Search, Email — Newsletter.
traffic_source_overrideSTRINGUser-set override (takes precedence over traffic_source when set).
activeBOOLEANWhether this rule is active; users can deactivate rules.
match_ll_platformSTRINGLL platform to match: google, meta, linkedin, tiktok, etc.
match_ll_platform_opSTRINGOperator for match_ll_platform comparison.
match_ll_mediumSTRINGLL medium to match: organic, paid.
match_ll_medium_opSTRINGOperator for match_ll_medium comparison.
match_ll_ad_typeSTRINGLL ad type to match: search, display, video, remarketing, native, etc.
match_ll_ad_type_opSTRINGOperator for match_ll_ad_type comparison.
match_utm_sourceSTRINGUTM source to match: google, facebook, newsletter, etc.
match_utm_source_opSTRINGOperator for match_utm_source: eq, ct (contains), sw (starts_with).
match_utm_mediumSTRINGUTM medium to match: cpc, email, organic, paid_social, etc.
match_utm_medium_opSTRINGOperator for match_utm_medium comparison.
match_referrer_hostSTRINGReferrer hostname to match, e.g. google., facebook.com.
match_referrer_host_opSTRINGOperator for referrer host match, usually ct (contains).
match_referrer_sameSTRINGSame-site referrer check: true, false, or NULL (skip).
match_click_id_fieldSTRINGBQ column name for click ID: gclid, fbclid, msclkid, ttclid, etc.
match_click_id_opSTRINGClick ID operator: ex (exists/non-null).
created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen the rule was created.
updated_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen the rule was last updated.
created_bySTRING NOT NULLWho created the rule: seed, llm, or manual.
is_defaultBOOLEANTrue for seed rules provisioned at account creation.

⬡phone_flows

Call flow configuration history — SCD Type 2. One row per configuration period per flow. Contains node-based routing logic (IVR menus, forwarding, AI agents, voicemail, ring groups) as JSON. When flow settings change, old row gets effective_until and new row is created. Source: settings-to-bigquery processor. Used for: understanding call routing at any point in time, flow performance analysis.

caller_identity.citySTRINGCity if detectable from area code.
caller_identity.phone_hashSTRINGSHA-256 of E.164 number — for matching to persons.phone_hash without exposing PII in downstream tables.
caller_identity.phone_hash_md5STRINGMD5 of E.164 — for legacy matching.
caller_identity.lookup_performedBOOLTRUE if number lookup was performed. FALSE if skipped (cached, rate-limited, or internal number).
caller_identity.lookup_cachedBOOLTRUE if result came from cache rather than live API call.
