call_screening.our_actionSTRINGAction our orchestration layer took based on account screening settings: allow, block, flag, flag_and_whisper. NULL when screening is disabled for the account.

triggers.conversionBOOLTRUE if any matched trigger is a conversion.

triggers.matches[].slugSTRINGHuman-readable trigger identifier.
triggers.matches[].is_conversionBOOLTRUE if this specific trigger is a conversion trigger.
data_actions.firedBOOLTRUE if any data actions fired.
data_actions.countINT64Number of data actions that fired.
data_actions.has_serverBOOLTRUE if any server-side data actions fired.
data_actions.actions[].action_idSTRINGData action UUID.
data_actions.actions[].slugSTRINGHuman-readable action identifier.
data_actions.actions[].typeSTRINGAction type: webhook, google_ads, meta_capi, etc.
data_actions.actions[].delivery_modeSTRINGserver (phone events are always server-side).
data_actions.actions[].trigger_idSTRINGTrigger that caused this action to fire.
data_actions.actions[].valueFLOAT64Conversion value sent with the action.

phone_conversions.firedBOOLTRUE if any conversions fired.

⬡phone_number_assignments

Phone number to web session mapping — built daily by the pipeline. Maps which tracking phone numbers were displayed to which web sessions by time window. Used for DNI (Dynamic Number Insertion) attribution: connecting inbound phone calls to the web session that displayed the tracking number. Source: daily pipeline processor from web_events phone assignment data. Used for: phone call attribution to web sessions.

Partition: assignment_dateCluster: account_id, phone_number, device_id
FieldTypeDescription
assignment_idSTRING NOT NULLGenerated UUID for this assignment record.
account_idSTRINGCustomer account ID.
phone_numberSTRING NOT NULLE.164 tracking number that was assigned.
tracking_number_idSTRINGFK to phone_numbers table.
pool_idSTRINGPool this number belongs to. Denormalized from phone_numbers for join efficiency.
device_idSTRING NOT NULLDevice that was shown this number.
session_idsARRAY\<STRING\>All session IDs active during this window. A device may have multiple sessions in one day.
event_sourceSTRINGweb \app — which platform displayed the number.
source_idSTRINGHostname or app_bundle_id where number was displayed.
assigned_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLFirst event timestamp with this number on this device in this window.
expires_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this assignment stops being valid for attribution. Computed as last_seen_at + assignment_ttl_minutes (TTL from account main settings). Capped at midnight if it would cross into the next day.
assignment_dateDATE NOT NULLDate of assignment. Partition key. Assignments spanning midnight produce two rows — one per day.
is_sharedBOOLTRUE when this number was simultaneously assigned to other devices during this window (pool exhausted). Multiple overlapping rows for the same number in the same time window indicates sharing. Calls during shared periods use the time-bucketed exclusivity + last-active resolution algorithm.
built_atTIMESTAMPWhen this assignment record was computed by the daily processor.

transcript.words[].end_msINT64Word end time in ms from recording start.
transcript.words[].confidenceFLOAT640-1 confidence for this word.
transcript.words[].speakerINT64Speaker index from diarization: 0, 1, 2. Map to caller/agent in post-processing.
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen this recording row was first created.
updated_atTIMESTAMPWhen this row was last updated (e.g., transcript added).

⬡crm_opportunity

CRM opportunity/deal records — current state. One row per deal. Contains amount, stage, close date, probability, forecast category, campaign source. The primary revenue object — Closed Won = conversion. Source: CRM via CDC or bulk import. Used for: pipeline analytics, revenue attribution, ROAS calculation, deal velocity analysis.

Partition: `close_date` (DATE)Cluster: `account_id`, `source_org_id`, `account_id_fk`
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system this record is from: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho. Enables blending records from multiple CRMs in the same table.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLInstance/org ID within the CRM type. SF org ID, HubSpot portal ID, Dynamics org GUID, etc. The pair (crm_type, source_org_id) uniquely identifies the connected CRM instance.
created_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF CreatedDate — when the record was created in Salesforce.
last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF LastModifiedDate — when any user last modified the record.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF SystemModstamp — system timestamp updated on any change including workflow/triggers. Used for sync watermarking.
is_deletedBOOL NOT NULLSF IsDeleted — true if record is in the Recycle Bin.
nameSTRINGOpportunity name. Free-form text, typically "Company - Product/Deal". Required on create in SF but nullable here for edge cases.
record_type_idSTRINGSF RecordTypeId. Only meaningful when Record Types are configured. Affects which StageName values are valid via Sales Process mapping.
record_type_nameSTRINGResolved record type label (e.g., "New Business", "Renewal"). Denormalized from RecordType object at sync time.
amountFLOAT64Estimated total sale amount. When line items exist, auto-calculated as sum of line item totals (read-only in SF). Stored in the record currency (see currency_iso_code).
expected_revenueFLOAT64SF-calculated: Amount * (Probability / 100). Read-only. NULL if Amount or Probability is null.
probabilityFLOAT64Likelihood of closing, 0-100. Auto-set from OpportunityStage.DefaultProbability when StageName changes, but can be manually overridden.
total_opportunity_quantityFLOAT64Total of all Quantity values on line items. When line items exist, auto-summed and read-only in SF.
currency_iso_codeSTRINGISO 4217 currency code (USD, EUR, GBP). FEATURE-GATED: only exists when Multi-Currency is enabled. NULL for single-currency orgs.
close_dateDATEExpected or actual close date. Required on create in SF. Drives fiscal field calculations.
last_activity_dateDATEDate of the most recent Task or Event logged against this opportunity. System-maintained by SF.
last_stage_change_dateTIMESTAMPWhen StageName was last changed. System-maintained. Available since SF API v47 (Winter 2020).
fiscalSTRINGFiscal period label, e.g., "2024 2". Derived from CloseDate + org Fiscal Year settings. NULL if Custom Fiscal Year is enabled.
fiscal_quarterINT64Fiscal quarter (1-4). Derived from CloseDate. NULL if Custom Fiscal Year is enabled.
fiscal_yearINT64Fiscal year (e.g., 2024). Derived from CloseDate. NULL if Custom Fiscal Year is enabled.
stage_nameSTRINGCurrent stage in the sales process. Values defined by OpportunityStage metadata. Required on create in SF. Changing this auto-updates Probability, ForecastCategory, IsClosed, and IsWon. JOIN to crm_opportunity_stages for stage metadata.
pipeline_nameSTRINGWhich pipeline this deal belongs to. Critical for HubSpot and Pipedrive (explicit multi-pipeline). For Salesforce, derived from RecordType or Sales Process. Enables pipeline-specific stage analysis when multiple pipelines are configured (e.g., New Business vs Renewals).
forecast_categorySTRINGInternal forecast category enum. Read-only — auto-set from StageName → OpportunityStage mapping. Values: Omitted, Pipeline, BestCase, MostLikely, Forecast, Closed. Legacy field — prefer forecast_category_name.
forecast_category_nameSTRINGUser-visible forecast category. Initially auto-set from StageName but CAN be manually overridden. Values: Omitted, Pipeline, Best Case, Most Likely, Commit, Closed. Use this over forecast_category.
has_overridden_forecast_categoryBOOLTRUE if forecast_category_name was manually overridden from the stage default. Useful for forecast accuracy analysis.
is_closedBOOLTRUE if the stage is a Closed stage (won or lost). Derived from OpportunityStage.IsClosed. Read-only.
is_wonBOOLTRUE if the stage is a Closed Won stage. Derived from OpportunityStage.IsWon. Read-only.
is_privateBOOLIf TRUE, only the Owner and admins can see the opportunity. Does not appear in reports/searches for other users.
typeSTRINGOpportunity type: New Customer, Existing Customer - Upgrade, Existing Customer - Replacement, Existing Customer - Downgrade, etc. Picklist.
lead_sourceSTRINGHow the opportunity originated. Carried from Lead.LeadSource during conversion. Standard values: Web, Phone Inquiry, Partner Referral, etc.
next_stepSTRINGDescription of the next step to advance the deal. Free-form text, max 255 chars.
descriptionSTRINGLong text area for opportunity details. Can be up to 32000 chars in SF.
account_id_fkSTRINGFK → crm_account.record_id. The Account this opportunity belongs to. Almost always populated.
account_nameSTRINGAccount/company name. Denormalized from crm_account.name at sync time. Saves JOIN for "deals by account" queries.
owner_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Owner of the opportunity. Defaults to the creating user. Can be a User or Queue.
created_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who created the record.
last_modified_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who last modified the record.
contact_idSTRINGFK → crm_contact.record_id. Primary contact on the opportunity. Auto-populated from Primary OpportunityContactRole. Added in SF API v46 (Spring 2019).
campaign_idSTRINGFK → crm_campaign.record_id. Primary Campaign Source. Auto-set from most recently associated campaign during Lead conversion.
contract_idSTRINGFK → crm_contract.record_id. Associated contract. FEATURE-GATED: only exists when Contracts are enabled.
pricebook_2_idSTRINGFK → SF Pricebook2. Price book for this opportunity. Required before adding line items.
synced_quote_idSTRINGFK → crm_quote.record_id. Currently synced quote. Only one can sync at a time. FEATURE-GATED: only exists when Quotes are enabled.
territory_2_idSTRINGFK → SF Territory2. Assigned territory. FEATURE-GATED: only exists when Enterprise Territory Management is enabled.
has_open_activityBOOLTRUE if the opportunity has at least one open (uncompleted) Task or Event. System-calculated by SF.
has_overdue_taskBOOLTRUE if the opportunity has at least one overdue Task. System-calculated by SF.
has_opportunity_line_itemBOOLTRUE if the opportunity has at least one product (OpportunityLineItem). When TRUE, Amount becomes auto-summed from line items.
push_countINT64Number of times CloseDate has been pushed to a later date. Only increments, never decrements. Introduced SF API v47.
last_amount_changed_history_idSTRINGFK → SF OpportunityHistory. Most recent history record where Amount changed.
last_close_date_changed_history_idSTRINGFK → SF OpportunityHistory. Most recent history record where CloseDate changed.
is_excluded_from_territory_2_filterBOOLWhen TRUE, excluded from filter-based territory assignment. FEATURE-GATED: Enterprise Territory Management only.
master_record_idSTRINGIf this record was merged into another, points to the surviving Opportunity record_id. Only populated on deleted/merged records.
ll_sales_statusSTRINGListenLayer unified status: open, won, lost. Normalized across CRMs (SF is_won/is_closed, HubSpot deal status, Dynamics StateCode). Deterministic — computed from CRM fields by rule.
ll_revenue.arrFLOAT64Annualized recurring revenue in the record currency. Computed from revenue config field_opinions — sums fields classified as recurring, annualized by period.
ll_revenue.arr_corporateFLOAT64ARR converted to corporate currency. Uses the CRM pre-converted corporate currency value (SF convertCurrency, HubSpot amount in company currency, Dynamics field_base). This is what gets summed in account rollups, pipeline, and LTV.
ll_revenue.mrrFLOAT64Monthly recurring revenue in record currency (arr / 12).
ll_revenue.mrr_corporateFLOAT64MRR in corporate currency (arr_corporate / 12).
ll_revenue.one_timeFLOAT64Non-recurring revenue in record currency. Sum of fields classified as one_time.
ll_revenue.one_time_corporateFLOAT64One-time revenue in corporate currency.
ll_revenue.tcvFLOAT64Total contract value in record currency (arr * term_years + one_time).
ll_revenue.tcv_corporateFLOAT64TCV in corporate currency.
ll_revenue.term_monthsINT64Contract/subscription term in months. From associated contract, line item dates, or revenue config default_term_months.
ll_revenue.revenue_typeSTRINGPrimary revenue classification: recurring, one_time, mixed, unknown. Based on which revenue config fields are populated.
ll_revenue.currency_iso_codeSTRINGRecord currency (ISO 4217). The currency of arr, mrr, one_time, tcv.
ll_revenue.corporate_currencySTRINGCorporate/base currency (ISO 4217). The currency of arr_corporate, mrr_corporate, one_time_corporate, tcv_corporate. From the CRM org settings.
ll_revenue.exchange_rateFLOAT64Exchange rate used for conversion (record currency → corporate currency). Stored for auditability. From CRM at time of computation.
ll_pipeline.stage_age_daysINT64Days in current stage. Computed from last_stage_change_date or crm_changelog stage transitions. NULL when is_closed = TRUE.
ll_pipeline.is_stalledBOOLTRUE if is_closed = FALSE AND stage_age_days exceeds 2x the historical average for this stage (from crm_opportunity_stages.ll_avg_days_in_stage). Always FALSE for closed deals.
ll_pipeline.ai_probabilityFLOAT64AI-assessed close probability (0.0-1.0). Based on historical stage conversion rates, deal signals, engagement. NULL when is_closed = TRUE.
ll_pipeline.ai_weighted_valueFLOAT64ll_revenue.arr_corporate * ai_probability. AI-corrected weighted pipeline value in corporate currency. NULL when is_closed = TRUE.
ll_pipeline.days_to_close_predictedINT64AI-predicted days until close. Based on historical velocity for similar deals at this stage. NULL when is_closed = TRUE or insufficient data.
intelligence.summarySTRINGAI-generated deal summary.
intelligence.deal_scoreFLOAT64AI assessment of deal health (0-100). Independent from SF Probability.
intelligence.risk_levelSTRINGDeal risk: low, medium, high, critical.
intelligence.signalsJSONArray of positive and negative signals: close_date_pushed, champion_went_quiet, strong_champion, multi_threaded, high_engagement, stalled_stage, etc.
intelligence.buying_stageSTRINGAI-assessed buying stage: early_research, active_evaluation, negotiation, decision. May differ from CRM stage.
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64Confidence in the analysis (0-1).
intelligence.last_analyzed_atTIMESTAMPWhen this intelligence was last generated.
custom_fieldsJSONAll org-specific custom fields (__c suffix) as a flat JSON object. Example: {"ARR__c": 50000, "MQL_Score__c": 85}. Query via JSON_VALUE(custom_fields, "$.Field__c"). Also stores any unknown fields from managed packages or future SF releases.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. How this row was last written: cdc, bulk_import, rest_fetch, deletion_sweep. Overwritten on each update.
_sync_batch_idSTRINGInternal. Which batch/job produced the last write. CDC batch checkpoint, Bulk API job ID, or REST sweep run ID. Overwritten on each update.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row. Overwritten on each update.
_first_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When we first ingested this record. Set once on first write, NEVER overwritten.
_cdc_replay_idSTRINGInternal. Pub/Sub replay ID from the last CDC event that updated this row. NULL for non-CDC sources. Overwritten on each CDC update.

⬡crm_contact

CRM contact/person records — current state. One row per contact. Contains name, email (identity resolution anchor — hashed and matched to persons table), phone, title, department, account relationship. Source: CRM via CDC or bulk import. Used for: identity resolution (email → persons), buying committee analysis, engagement tracking.

Partition: `DATE(last_modified_date)`Cluster: `account_id`, `source_org_id`, `account_id_fk`
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system this record is from: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho. Enables blending records from multiple CRMs in the same table.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLInstance/org ID within the CRM type. SF org ID, HubSpot portal ID, Dynamics org GUID, etc. The pair (crm_type, source_org_id) uniquely identifies the connected CRM instance.
created_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF CreatedDate — when the record was created in Salesforce.
last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF LastModifiedDate — when any user last modified the record.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF SystemModstamp — system timestamp, used for sync watermarking and last-modstamp-wins.
is_deletedBOOL NOT NULLSF IsDeleted — true if record is in the Recycle Bin.
record_type_idSTRINGSF RecordTypeId. Only meaningful when Record Types are configured.
record_type_nameSTRINGResolved record type label. Denormalized at sync time.
master_record_idSTRINGIf this record was merged into another, points to the surviving Contact record_id. Only populated on deleted/merged records.
is_person_accountBOOLTRUE if this Contact is the auto-created contact for a Person Account. Used to flag these in identity resolution to avoid double-counting with crm_account. Feature-gated: Person Accounts.
salutationSTRINGSalutation picklist: Mr., Ms., Mrs., Dr., Prof., etc.
first_nameSTRINGFirst/given name.
middle_nameSTRINGMiddle name. Feature-gated: requires "Middle Name for Person Names" enabled.
last_nameSTRINGLast/family name. The ONLY required field on create in SF.
suffixSTRINGName suffix: Jr., Sr., III, etc. Feature-gated: requires "Name Suffix" enabled.
emailSTRINGPrimary email address. KEY FIELD for identity resolution — hashed and matched to persons.email_hash.
phoneSTRINGPrimary business phone.
mobile_phoneSTRINGMobile/cell number.
home_phoneSTRINGHome phone number.
other_phoneSTRINGOther/alternate phone.
faxSTRINGFax number.
assistant_phoneSTRINGAssistant phone number.
assistant_nameSTRINGName of the contact assistant.
titleSTRINGJob title / designation.
departmentSTRINGDepartment within the company.
lead_sourceSTRINGHow this contact/lead originated. Carried from Lead.LeadSource on conversion. Standard values: Web, Phone Inquiry, Partner Referral, etc.
lifecycle_stageSTRINGContact qualification stage in the funnel. Critical for HubSpot (Subscriber, Lead, MQL, SQL, Opportunity, Customer, Evangelist). For Salesforce, derived from Lead conversion status or custom field. For Pipedrive, derived from deal associations. Enables cross-CRM funnel analysis.
birthdateDATEDate of birth. Date only (no time component). Commonly populated in B2C contexts.
descriptionSTRINGLong text description/notes. Up to 32000 chars.
has_opted_out_of_emailBOOLTRUE = contact has opted out of email. Platform-enforced for mass emails. Critical for identity resolution — affects outreach eligibility.
has_opted_out_of_faxBOOLTRUE = opted out of fax. Advisory flag, not platform-enforced.
do_not_callBOOLTRUE = do not call. Advisory flag, not platform-enforced.
individual_idSTRINGFK → SF Individual object for GDPR/CCPA consent preferences. Feature-gated: Data Protection and Privacy.
email_bounced_reasonSTRINGReason string from the email bounce (e.g., "550 User unknown").
email_bounced_dateTIMESTAMPWhen the email bounce was recorded.
mailing_address.streetSTRINGStreet address (supports multi-line).
mailing_address.citySTRINGCity.
mailing_address.stateSTRINGState or province name.
mailing_address.postal_codeSTRINGZIP or postal code.
mailing_address.countrySTRINGCountry name.
mailing_address.state_codeSTRINGISO state code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
mailing_address.country_codeSTRINGISO country code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
mailing_address.latitudeFLOAT64Latitude (-90 to 90). Feature-gated: geocoding.
mailing_address.longitudeFLOAT64Longitude (-180 to 180). Feature-gated: geocoding.
mailing_address.geocode_accuracySTRINGGeocode accuracy level. Feature-gated: Data Integration Rules.
other_address.streetSTRINGStreet address.
other_address.citySTRINGCity.
other_address.stateSTRINGState or province name.
other_address.postal_codeSTRINGZIP or postal code.
other_address.countrySTRINGCountry name.
other_address.state_codeSTRINGISO state code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
other_address.country_codeSTRINGISO country code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
other_address.latitudeFLOAT64Latitude.
other_address.longitudeFLOAT64Longitude.
other_address.geocode_accuracySTRINGGeocode accuracy level. Feature-gated: Data Integration Rules.
account_id_fkSTRINGFK → crm_account.record_id. The primary Account this Contact belongs to. NULL = private contact (visible only to owner). For multi-account relationships, see crm_account_contact_relations.
owner_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Owner of the contact record.
created_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who created the record.
last_modified_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who last modified the record.
reports_to_idSTRINGFK → crm_contact.record_id (self-referencing). Org chart / reporting hierarchy. Rarely populated in practice.
last_activity_dateDATEDate of the last completed Task or Event. System-maintained.
ll_company_domainSTRINGListenLayer computed: company domain extracted from email field (after @, excluding free email providers like gmail.com, yahoo.com, outlook.com). E.g., "john@acme.com" → "acme.com". NULL if email is a free provider or empty.
ll_is_qualifiedBOOLListenLayer computed: contact meets qualification criteria. Rule defined in account_settings.crm_computed_rules. AI generates on connect, customer approves.
ll_qualified_atTIMESTAMPWhen the qualification rule first matched. Set once, never overwritten. Transition recorded in crm_changelog.
ll_is_customerBOOLListenLayer computed: inherited from the associated Account (ll_is_customer on crm_account). TRUE if the primary account is a customer.
ll_customer_sinceTIMESTAMPInherited from associated Account. When the account became a customer.
ll_sales_statusSTRINGListenLayer unified status: new, working, qualified, customer, churned. Deterministic — computed from CRM fields by rule.
intelligence.summarySTRINGAI-generated contact summary.
intelligence.engagement_scoreFLOAT64Contact engagement level (0-100). Considers web visits, meetings, form submissions, CRM activity.
intelligence.buyer_roleSTRINGAI-assessed buying role: champion, decision_maker, influencer, evaluator, blocker, end_user, unknown.
intelligence.journey_stageSTRINGBuying journey position: awareness, consideration, evaluation, decision, customer, advocate.
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64Confidence in the analysis (0-1).
intelligence.last_analyzed_atTIMESTAMPWhen this intelligence was last generated.
custom_fieldsJSONAll org-specific custom fields (__c suffix), legacy Data.com fields, and unknown/managed-package fields as a flat JSON object.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. How this row was last written: cdc, bulk_import, rest_fetch, deletion_sweep. Overwritten on each update.
_sync_batch_idSTRINGInternal. Which batch/job produced the last write. Overwritten on each update.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row. Overwritten on each update.
_first_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When we first ingested this record. Set once, NEVER overwritten.
_cdc_replay_idSTRINGInternal. Last Pub/Sub replay ID that updated this row. NULL for non-CDC sources. Overwritten on each CDC update.

⬡crm_lead

CRM lead/prospect records — current state. One row per lead. Leads persist after conversion (is_converted=true, never deleted). Conversion fields link to resulting Account/Contact/Opportunity. Source: CRM via CDC or bulk import. Used for: lead funnel analysis, conversion tracking, identity resolution (email → persons).

Partition: `DATE(created_date)`Cluster: `account_id`, `source_org_id`, `status`, `is_converted`
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system this record is from: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLInstance/org ID within the CRM type. The pair (crm_type, source_org_id) uniquely identifies the connected CRM instance.
created_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF CreatedDate — when the record was created in Salesforce.
last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF LastModifiedDate — when any user last modified the record.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF SystemModstamp — system timestamp, used for sync watermarking and last-modstamp-wins.
is_deletedBOOL NOT NULLSF IsDeleted — true if record is in the Recycle Bin.
record_type_idSTRINGSF RecordTypeId. Only meaningful when Record Types are configured.
record_type_nameSTRINGResolved record type label. Denormalized at sync time.
master_record_idSTRINGIf this record was merged into another, points to the surviving Lead record_id. Only populated on deleted/merged records.
salutationSTRINGSalutation picklist: Mr., Ms., Mrs., Dr., Prof., etc.
first_nameSTRINGFirst/given name.
last_nameSTRINGLast/family name. Required on create in SF.
suffixSTRINGName suffix: Jr., Sr., III, etc. Feature-gated: requires "Name Suffix" enabled.
titleSTRINGJob title / designation.
companySTRINGCompany/organization name. Required on create in SF. Maps to Account.Name on conversion.
industrySTRINGIndustry picklist: Agriculture, Banking, Technology, etc.
annual_revenueFLOAT64Annual revenue of the lead company. Stored in the record currency (see currency_iso_code).
number_of_employeesINT64Number of employees at the lead company.
emailSTRINGLead email address. KEY FIELD for identity resolution — hashed and matched to persons.email_hash.
phoneSTRINGPrimary phone number.
mobile_phoneSTRINGMobile/cell number.
faxSTRINGFax number.
websiteSTRINGLead company website URL.
statusSTRINGLead lifecycle status picklist. Required on create in SF. Values defined by LeadStatus metadata (e.g., Open, Working, Closed-Converted, Closed-Not Converted). JOIN to crm_lead_statuses for IsConverted flag per status.
ratingSTRINGLead quality rating picklist: Hot, Warm, Cold.
lead_sourceSTRINGHow this lead originated: Web, Phone Inquiry, Partner Referral, Purchased List, Other. Carried to Contact.LeadSource and Opportunity.LeadSource on conversion.
is_convertedBOOLTRUE if the lead has been converted. Once true, cannot revert. The lead becomes read-only. Still queryable — filtered out of list views by default.
converted_dateDATEDate when the lead was converted. DATE only (no time component).
converted_account_idSTRINGFK → crm_account.record_id. The Account created or merged into during conversion. Always populated after conversion.
converted_contact_idSTRINGFK → crm_contact.record_id. The Contact created during conversion. Always populated after conversion.
converted_opportunity_idSTRINGFK → crm_opportunity.record_id. The Opportunity created during conversion. NULL if doNotCreateOpportunity was true.
address.streetSTRINGStreet address (supports multi-line).
address.citySTRINGCity.
address.stateSTRINGState or province name.
address.postal_codeSTRINGZIP or postal code.
address.countrySTRINGCountry name.
address.state_codeSTRINGISO state code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
address.country_codeSTRINGISO country code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
address.latitudeFLOAT64Latitude (-90 to 90). Feature-gated: geocoding.
address.longitudeFLOAT64Longitude (-180 to 180). Feature-gated: geocoding.
address.geocode_accuracySTRINGGeocode accuracy level. Feature-gated: Data Integration Rules.
email_bounced_reasonSTRINGReason string from the email bounce (e.g., "550 User unknown").
email_bounced_dateTIMESTAMPWhen the email bounce was recorded.
owner_idSTRINGFK → SF User or Queue. Owner of the lead. CAN be a Queue (unlike Account). Queue IDs start with 00G, User IDs with 005.
created_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who created the record.
last_modified_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who last modified the record.
is_unread_by_ownerBOOLTRUE if the owner has not yet viewed the lead after assignment. Auto-set true on ownership change, false when owner views/edits.
last_activity_dateDATEDate of the last completed Task or Event. System-maintained.
descriptionSTRINGLong text description/notes. Up to 32000 chars.
currency_iso_codeSTRINGISO 4217 currency code (USD, EUR, GBP). FEATURE-GATED: only exists when Multi-Currency is enabled. Applies to annual_revenue field.
ll_company_domainSTRINGListenLayer computed: company domain extracted from email field (after @, excluding free providers) OR from website field if present. Lead has both sources — email domain is primary, website is fallback.
ll_is_qualifiedBOOLListenLayer computed: lead meets qualification criteria. Rule defined in account_settings.crm_computed_rules. AI generates on connect, customer approves.
ll_qualified_atTIMESTAMPWhen the qualification rule first matched. Set once, never overwritten. Transition recorded in crm_changelog.
ll_sales_statusSTRINGListenLayer unified status: new, working, qualified, converted, disqualified. Deterministic — computed from CRM fields by rule.
intelligence.summarySTRINGAI-generated lead summary.
intelligence.quality_scoreFLOAT64AI assessment of lead quality (0-100). Considers ICP fit, engagement, data completeness, source quality.
intelligence.conversion_likelihoodFLOAT64Predicted probability of converting to opportunity (0-1). Based on historical patterns.
intelligence.icp_fitSTRINGICP fit: strong_fit, good_fit, partial_fit, poor_fit.
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64Confidence in the analysis (0-1).
intelligence.last_analyzed_atTIMESTAMPWhen this intelligence was last generated.
custom_fieldsJSONAll org-specific custom fields (__c suffix), legacy Data.com fields, Sales Engagement fields, IndividualId, and unknown/managed-package fields as a flat JSON object.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. How this row was last written: cdc, bulk_import, rest_fetch, deletion_sweep. Overwritten on each update.
_sync_batch_idSTRINGInternal. Which batch/job produced the last write. Overwritten on each update.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row. Overwritten on each update.
_first_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When we first ingested this record. Set once, NEVER overwritten.
_cdc_replay_idSTRINGInternal. Last Pub/Sub replay ID that updated this row. NULL for non-CDC sources. Overwritten on each CDC update.

⬡crm_account

CRM account/company records — current state. One row per account, overwritten on each change. Contains company name, industry, revenue, employees, website (domain matching anchor to companies table), billing/shipping addresses, account hierarchy (parent_id). Source: Salesforce, HubSpot, Dynamics, or Pipedrive via CDC or bulk import. Used for: company-level analytics, revenue roll-up, account health scoring.

Partition: `DATE(last_modified_date)`Cluster: `account_id`, `source_org_id`, `parent_id`
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system this record is from: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho. Enables blending records from multiple CRMs in the same table.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLInstance/org ID within the CRM type. SF org ID, HubSpot portal ID, Dynamics org GUID, etc. The pair (crm_type, source_org_id) uniquely identifies the connected CRM instance.
created_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF CreatedDate — when the record was created in Salesforce.
last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF LastModifiedDate — when any user last modified the record.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSF SystemModstamp — system timestamp, used for sync watermarking and last-modstamp-wins.
is_deletedBOOL NOT NULLSF IsDeleted — true if record is in the Recycle Bin.
nameSTRINGAccount name. Required on create for Business Accounts. For Person Accounts, auto-composed from FirstName + LastName (read-only).
record_type_idSTRINGSF RecordTypeId. Controls Business Account vs Person Account when Person Accounts are enabled. Affects picklist values.
record_type_nameSTRINGResolved record type label. Denormalized at sync time.
master_record_idSTRINGIf this record was merged into another, points to the surviving Account record_id. Only populated on deleted/merged records. Essential for merge tracking.
is_person_accountBOOLTRUE if this is a Person Account (Account + Contact merged). FALSE or NULL for Business Accounts. Determines whether Person-prefixed fields exist on this record.
person_contact_idSTRINGFK → crm_contact.record_id. The underlying Contact record auto-created for Person Accounts. NULL for Business Accounts.
typeSTRINGAccount type picklist: Analyst, Competitor, Customer, Integrator, Investor, Partner, Press, Prospect, Reseller, Other. Customizable per org.
industrySTRINGPrimary industry picklist: Agriculture, Banking, Biotechnology, Communications, Construction, Consulting, Education, Electronics, Energy, Finance, Government, Healthcare, Hospitality, Insurance, Manufacturing, Media, Retail, Technology, Telecommunications, Transportation, Utilities, Other, etc.
ratingSTRINGAccount rating/priority picklist: Hot, Warm, Cold.
ownershipSTRINGOwnership type picklist: Public, Private, Subsidiary, Other.
account_sourceSTRINGSource of the account record. Shares picklist values with Lead.LeadSource. Carried from Lead.LeadSource on conversion.
annual_revenueFLOAT64Estimated annual revenue. Stored in the record currency (see currency_iso_code).
number_of_employeesINT64Number of employees.
ticker_symbolSTRINGStock ticker symbol (e.g., AAPL, MSFT).
siteSTRINGAccount location name (e.g., "HQ", "London Office"). For multi-location companies. Not a URL — see website.
year_startedSTRINGYear the company was established. Stored as 4-char string, not a date.
tradestyleSTRINGDBA (Doing Business As) or trade name.
account_numberSTRINGCustomer-defined account number. Free-text, not auto-generated. Often an ERP or billing system ID.
descriptionSTRINGLong text description of the account. Up to 32000 chars.
sicSTRINGStandard Industrial Classification code. 4-digit industry code.
sic_descSTRINGSIC code description (e.g., "Prepackaged Software").
naics_codeSTRINGNorth American Industry Classification System code. 2-6 digits.
naics_descSTRINGNAICS code description.
phoneSTRINGPrimary phone number.
faxSTRINGFax number.
websiteSTRINGCompany website URL. KEY FIELD for domain matching — used to resolve this Account to our companies table via domain extraction.
billing_address.streetSTRINGStreet address (supports multi-line).
billing_address.citySTRINGCity.
billing_address.stateSTRINGState or province name.
billing_address.postal_codeSTRINGZIP or postal code.
billing_address.countrySTRINGCountry name.
billing_address.state_codeSTRINGISO state code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
billing_address.country_codeSTRINGISO country code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
billing_address.latitudeFLOAT64Latitude (-90 to 90). Feature-gated: geocoding.
billing_address.longitudeFLOAT64Longitude (-180 to 180). Feature-gated: geocoding.
billing_address.geocode_accuracySTRINGGeocode accuracy level: Address, NearAddress, Block, Street, Zip, City, State, Country. Feature-gated: Data Integration Rules.
shipping_address.streetSTRINGStreet address.
shipping_address.citySTRINGCity.
shipping_address.stateSTRINGState or province name.
shipping_address.postal_codeSTRINGZIP or postal code.
shipping_address.countrySTRINGCountry name.
shipping_address.state_codeSTRINGISO state code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
shipping_address.country_codeSTRINGISO country code. Feature-gated: State & Country picklists.
shipping_address.latitudeFLOAT64Latitude.
shipping_address.longitudeFLOAT64Longitude.
shipping_address.geocode_accuracySTRINGGeocode accuracy level. Feature-gated: Data Integration Rules.
parent_idSTRINGFK → crm_account.record_id (self-referencing). Creates the account hierarchy tree. No enforced depth limit in SF. Use BigQuery recursive CTEs to traverse.
owner_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Owner of the account. Cannot be a Queue (Accounts only support User ownership, unlike Leads/Cases).
created_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who created the record.
last_modified_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who last modified the record.
last_activity_dateDATEDate of the last completed Task or Event. Rolled up from child activities. System-maintained.
currency_iso_codeSTRINGISO 4217 currency code (USD, EUR, GBP). FEATURE-GATED: only exists when Multi-Currency is enabled.
ll_company_domainSTRINGListenLayer computed: normalized company domain extracted from the website field. Strips protocol, www, path — just the domain (e.g., "acme.com"). This is the JOIN key to companies.domain for identity resolution.
ll_is_customerBOOLListenLayer computed: account meets customer criteria. Rule defined in account_settings.crm_computed_rules. AI generates the rule on connect, customer approves.
ll_customer_sinceTIMESTAMPWhen the customer rule first matched. Set once, never overwritten. Transition recorded in crm_changelog.
ll_is_churnedBOOLListenLayer computed: was a customer but no longer meets criteria. Only true if ll_is_customer was previously true.
ll_churned_atTIMESTAMPWhen churn was detected. Transition recorded in crm_changelog.
ll_sales_statusSTRINGListenLayer unified status: prospect, customer, churned, partner, other. Deterministic — computed from CRM fields by rule.
ll_revenue.current_arrFLOAT64Current ARR in corporate currency. Sum of ll_revenue.arr_corporate from won opportunities WHERE is_won = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE. 0.0 if churned. NULL if never had revenue.
ll_revenue.current_mrrFLOAT64Current MRR in corporate currency (current_arr / 12).
ll_revenue.total_revenueFLOAT64All-time total revenue in corporate currency (recurring + one-time) across all won deals. Cumulative, never decreases.
ll_revenue.first_revenue_atTIMESTAMPTimestamp of first won deal close_date. Defines customer cohort entry. Never changes once set, even after churn.
ll_revenue.last_revenue_atTIMESTAMPTimestamp of most recent won deal close_date. Recency indicator.
ll_revenue.ltv_to_dateFLOAT64Cumulative lifetime value in corporate currency. Sum of all recognized revenue. Never decreases — refunds not subtracted.
ll_revenue.corporate_currencySTRINGCorporate/base currency (ISO 4217) of all monetary values in this STRUCT. From CRM org settings.
intelligence.summarySTRINGAI-generated account summary.
intelligence.health_scoreFLOAT64Overall account health (0-100). Considers revenue trend, deals, cases, engagement, renewal proximity.
intelligence.health_statusSTRINGAccount health: thriving, healthy, at_risk, churning, dormant, new.
intelligence.churn_riskSTRINGChurn risk level: low, medium, high, critical.
intelligence.churn_signalsJSONAI-identified churn risk factors as JSON array. E.g., ["contract_expiring_soon", "revenue_declining", "no_recent_activity"]. NULL if churn_risk is low.
intelligence.predicted_churn_atDATEAI-predicted churn date if churn_risk is high or critical. NULL if churn_risk is low/medium.
intelligence.revenue_trendSTRINGAI-assessed revenue direction: growing, stable, contracting, churning.
intelligence.expansion_potentialSTRINGExpansion assessment: high, medium, low, none.
intelligence.next_renewal_dateDATENearest contract end date or subscription renewal. NULL if no contracts.
intelligence.icp_fitSTRINGICP fit: strong_fit, good_fit, partial_fit, poor_fit.
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64Confidence in the analysis (0-1).
intelligence.last_analyzed_atTIMESTAMPWhen this intelligence was last generated.
custom_fieldsJSONAll org-specific custom fields (__c suffix), Person Account fields (PersonEmail, PersonTitle, PersonMobilePhone, PersonMailingAddress, etc.), managed package fields, and legacy/unknown fields as a flat JSON object.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. How this row was last written: cdc, bulk_import, rest_fetch, deletion_sweep. Overwritten on each update.
_sync_batch_idSTRINGInternal. Which batch/job produced the last write. Overwritten on each update.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row. Overwritten on each update.
_first_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When we first ingested this record. Set once, NEVER overwritten.
_cdc_replay_idSTRINGInternal. Last Pub/Sub replay ID that updated this row. NULL for non-CDC sources. Overwritten on each CDC update.

⬡crm_campaign

CRM marketing campaign records — current state. Marketing programs with hierarchy (max 5 levels), budget/cost tracking, 20 auto-calculated roll-up statistics. Intelligence STRUCT classifies campaign category and assesses performance. Source: CRM via CDC or bulk import. Used for: marketing attribution, campaign ROI, program analysis.

Partition: `start_date` (DATE)Cluster: `account_id`, `source_org_id`, `type`, `is_active`
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system this record is from: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLInstance/org ID within the CRM type.
created_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this campaign was created.
last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen last modified.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSystem timestamp for sync watermarking.
is_deletedBOOL NOT NULLTrue if record is deleted.
nameSTRINGCampaign name. Required on create in SF.
record_type_idSTRINGSF RecordTypeId. Common pattern: different record types for Email, Event, Paid Media, Webinar campaigns.
record_type_nameSTRINGResolved record type label. Denormalized at sync time.
typeSTRINGCampaign type picklist: Advertising, Direct Mail, Email, Telemarketing, Seminar/Conference, Public Relations, Partners, Referral Program, Other. Heavily customized per org.
statusSTRINGCampaign lifecycle status picklist: Planned, In Progress, Completed, Aborted. Purely informational — no automation tied to it. Independent from is_active.
is_activeBOOLWhether campaign is active. When false: excluded from lookup searches, new members cannot be added via UI. Independent from status.
start_dateDATECampaign start date. Date only (no time).
end_dateDATECampaign end date. Date only. No validation that end > start.
descriptionSTRINGCampaign description. Up to 32000 chars.
parent_idSTRINGFK → crm_campaign.record_id (self-referencing). Campaign hierarchy — max 5 levels deep (SF hard limit). Hierarchy roll-up fields aggregate across all descendants.
budgeted_costFLOAT64Planned/forecasted campaign spend. Stored in campaign currency.
actual_costFLOAT64Actual campaign spend. Stored in campaign currency.
expected_revenueFLOAT64Projected revenue from campaign. Stored in campaign currency.
number_sentFLOAT64Number of individuals targeted (e.g., emails sent, mailers posted). Manually set — not auto-calculated.
expected_responseFLOAT64Expected response rate as percentage (0-100).
number_of_leadsINT64Count of Leads in this campaign (via CampaignMember). Read-only.
number_of_converted_leadsINT64Count of converted Leads. Read-only.
number_of_contactsINT64Count of Contacts in this campaign. Read-only.
number_of_responsesINT64Count of members with HasResponded=true. Read-only.
number_of_opportunitiesINT64Count of Opportunities with this as Primary Campaign Source. Read-only.
number_of_won_opportunitiesINT64Count of Closed Won Opportunities. Read-only.
amount_all_opportunitiesFLOAT64Sum of Amount on all linked Opportunities. Read-only. In campaign currency.
amount_won_opportunitiesFLOAT64Sum of Amount on won Opportunities. Read-only. In campaign currency.
hierarchy_number_of_leadsINT64Total Leads across this campaign + all descendants. Read-only.
hierarchy_number_of_converted_leadsINT64Total converted Leads in hierarchy. Read-only.
hierarchy_number_of_contactsINT64Total Contacts in hierarchy. Read-only.
hierarchy_number_of_responsesINT64Total Responses in hierarchy. Read-only.
hierarchy_number_of_opportunitiesINT64Total Opportunities in hierarchy. Read-only.
hierarchy_number_of_won_opportunitiesINT64Total won Opportunities in hierarchy. Read-only.
hierarchy_amount_all_opportunitiesFLOAT64Sum of all Opportunity amounts in hierarchy. Read-only. Converted to parent campaign currency.
hierarchy_amount_won_opportunitiesFLOAT64Sum of won Opportunity amounts in hierarchy. Read-only.
hierarchy_number_sentFLOAT64Total NumberSent across hierarchy. Read-only.
hierarchy_expected_revenueFLOAT64Total ExpectedRevenue across hierarchy. Read-only.
hierarchy_budgeted_costFLOAT64Total BudgetedCost across hierarchy. Read-only.
hierarchy_actual_costFLOAT64Total ActualCost across hierarchy. Read-only.
owner_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Campaign owner.
created_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who created the record.
last_modified_by_idSTRINGFK → SF User. Who last modified the record.
campaign_member_record_type_idSTRINGDefault RecordType for CampaignMember records created under this campaign.
last_activity_dateDATEDate of the last Task or Event. System-maintained.
currency_iso_codeSTRINGISO 4217 currency code. FEATURE-GATED: Multi-Currency only. Applies to BudgetedCost, ActualCost, ExpectedRevenue, and all currency roll-ups.
intelligence.categorySTRINGAI-classified campaign category: paid_search, paid_social, organic_social, email, webinar, event_in_person, event_virtual, content, direct_mail, referral, partner, nurture, abm, product_led, other.

intelligence.performance_assessmentSTRINGHow the campaign is performing relative to similar campaigns: strong, average, underperforming, too_early_to_tell.
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64Confidence in the classification (0-1).
intelligence.last_analyzed_atTIMESTAMPWhen this intelligence was last generated/refreshed.
custom_fieldsJSONAll org-specific custom fields. Campaigns are heavily customized — UTM alignment, channel/program classification, ad platform integration IDs, ROI formulas, targeting fields.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. How this row was last written: cdc, bulk_import, rest_fetch, deletion_sweep.

⬡crm_campaign_members

CRM campaign membership junction — links campaigns to leads and contacts. One row per member. Polymorphic (lead_id OR contact_id). Tracks response status and first_responded_date (key attribution timestamp). CRITICAL: no CDC support in Salesforce — synced via Bulk API polling. Source: CRM APIs. Used for: multi-touch marketing attribution chain.

Partition: `DATE(created_date)`Cluster: `account_id`, `source_org_id`, `campaign_id`, `contact_id`
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).

crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system this record is from: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLInstance/org ID within the CRM type.
created_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this member was ADDED to the campaign. Not the date of engagement — see first_responded_date for that.
last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen last modified.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSystem timestamp for sync watermarking.
is_deletedBOOL NOT NULLTrue if record is deleted.

lead_idSTRINGFK → crm_lead.record_id. Populated when member is a Lead. Cleared on Lead conversion (contact_id gets set instead). NULL when member is a Contact.
contact_idSTRINGFK → crm_contact.record_id. Populated when member is a Contact, or when a Lead member converts. NULL when member is a Lead.
member_typeSTRINGResolved type: "Lead" or "Contact". Derived from which FK is populated. After Lead conversion, changes to "Contact".
statusSTRINGMember status within this campaign. Values are per-campaign (defined in crm_campaign_member_statuses). Default: "Sent", "Responded". Customizable per campaign.

first_responded_dateDATEWhen HasResponded first became TRUE. Set once, NEVER overwritten. This is the KEY attribution timestamp: when the person actually engaged with the campaign.

⬡crm_campaign_influence

CRM campaign influence junction — links campaigns to opportunities with revenue credit per attribution model. The most important marketing attribution table. Multiple records per campaign-opportunity pair (one per model). Always filter by model_id to avoid double-counting. Source: CRM APIs. Used for: multi-touch revenue attribution, campaign ROI.

Partition: noneCluster: `account_id`, `source_org_id`, `opportunity_id`, `campaign_id`
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system this record is from.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLInstance/org ID within the CRM type.
created_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this influence record was created.
last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen last modified.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSystem timestamp for sync watermarking.
is_deletedBOOL NOT NULLTrue if record is deleted.
campaign_idSTRINGFK → crm_campaign.record_id. The influencing campaign.
opportunity_idSTRINGFK → crm_opportunity.record_id. The influenced opportunity.
contact_idSTRINGFK → crm_contact.record_id. The bridging Contact — was a CampaignMember AND had an OpportunityContactRole. This is how the influence chain is established.
model_idSTRINGFK → crm_campaign_influence_models.record_id. Which attribution model produced this record. CRITICAL: always filter by model_id to avoid double-counting — multiple records exist per Campaign-Opportunity pair (one per model).
influenceFLOAT64Influence percentage (0-100) assigned by this model. Within a single model, percentages for one Opportunity should sum to 100%. Primary Campaign Source model: 100% to primary, 0% to others. Even Distribution: equal split.
revenueFLOAT64Revenue credit: Influence% × Opportunity.Amount. Read-only — auto-calculated by SF. Recalculates when Influence% or Opportunity.Amount changes. In Opportunity currency.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. How this row was last written.
_sync_batch_idSTRINGInternal. Which batch/job produced the last write.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.
_first_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When we first ingested this record. Never overwritten.
_cdc_replay_idSTRINGInternal. Last Pub/Sub replay ID. NULL for non-CDC sources.

⬡crm_case

CRM support case/ticket records — current state. One row per case. Contains priority, status, origin channel (email/phone/web/chat), SLA tracking, case hierarchy. Intelligence STRUCT assesses severity and churn risk. Source: CRM via CDC or bulk import. Used for: support analytics, churn prediction, customer health scoring.

Partition: `DATE(created_date)`Cluster: `account_id`, `source_org_id`, `account_id_fk`, `status`
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).

record_type_nameSTRINGResolved record type label. Denormalized at sync time.

prioritySTRINGCase priority picklist: High, Medium, Low. Some orgs add Critical/Urgent. Drives SLA milestone timing.

intelligence.churn_risk_signalSTRINGChurn risk from this case: high, medium, low, none.

⬡crm_opportunity_stages

CRM opportunity stage definitions — maps each stage to default probability, forecast category, is_won, is_closed, and sort order. Tiny dimension table (5-20 rows). Without this table you cannot determine which stages represent won deals. Source: CRM Metadata API, refreshed daily. Used for: pipeline reporting, stage-to-forecast mapping.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, source_org_id
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID or synthetic key. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLInstance/org ID within the CRM type.
created_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this metadata was created.
last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen last modified.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSystem timestamp for sync watermarking.
is_deletedBOOL NOT NULLTrue if stage has been removed.
master_labelSTRINGStage display name (e.g., "Prospecting", "Qualification", "Proposal", "Negotiation", "Closed Won", "Closed Lost").
api_nameSTRINGStage API name. Usually same as MasterLabel.
default_probabilityFLOAT64Default probability (0-100) assigned to Opportunities when this stage is selected. Auto-populates Opportunity.Probability.
forecast_categorySTRINGInternal forecast category: Omitted, Pipeline, BestCase, MostLikely, Forecast, Closed.
forecast_category_nameSTRINGUser-visible forecast category: Omitted, Pipeline, Best Case, Most Likely, Commit, Closed.
is_activeBOOLWhether this stage is currently available for use.
is_closedBOOLTRUE if this stage represents a closed deal (won OR lost). Drives Opportunity.IsClosed.
is_wonBOOLTRUE if this stage represents a won deal (Closed Won). Drives Opportunity.IsWon. FALSE for Closed Lost stages.
sort_orderINT64Display order in the stage picklist. Defines the pipeline progression sequence.
ll_conversion_rateFLOAT64Historical win rate: % of deals that enter this stage and eventually close won (is_won = TRUE). Excludes deleted deals. Computed from crm_changelog stage transitions. E.g., 0.35 means 35% of deals that reach this stage eventually win. Recomputed daily.
ll_conversion_sample_sizeINT64Number of closed deals (won + lost) this conversion rate is based on. Low values (< 20) mean the rate is unreliable. NULL if no closed deals have passed through this stage.
ll_avg_days_in_stageFLOAT64Average days deals spend in this stage before advancing or closing. Computed from crm_changelog stage transitions for closed deals only (open deals would skew the average). Used to detect stalled deals (open deals exceeding 2x this value are flagged).
ll_drop_rateFLOAT64% of deals that go directly from this stage to Closed Lost without advancing to the next stage. High drop rates indicate where deals die. Excludes deleted deals.
ll_computed_atTIMESTAMPWhen these computed fields were last updated.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. Always "metadata_api" or "soql_query" for this table.
_sync_batch_idSTRINGInternal. Which sync run produced the last write.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.
_first_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When we first ingested this record. Never overwritten.
_cdc_replay_idSTRINGInternal. Always NULL — this table is not CDC-synced.

⬡crm_products

CRM product catalog — reference/dimension table. One row per product. Contains name, code, family (critical for product grouping analytics), SKU, active status. Typically 50-5000 rows. Source: CRM via CDC. Used for: JOIN target for all line item tables, product mix reporting.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, source_org_id
FieldTypeDescription
record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. Primary key within (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id).
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID. Set by our pipeline.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system this record is from: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLInstance/org ID within the CRM type.
created_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this product was created.
last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen last modified.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSystem timestamp for sync watermarking.
is_deletedBOOL NOT NULLTrue if record is deleted.
nameSTRINGProduct name (e.g., "Enterprise License", "Professional Services").
product_codeSTRINGProduct SKU/code. Customer-defined identifier.
familySTRINGProduct family picklist — CRITICAL for product analytics grouping. Values like: Software, Hardware, Services, Support, Add-On. Customizable per org.
is_activeBOOLTRUE if the product is currently active/available for sale. FALSE for retired products.
quantity_unit_of_measureSTRINGUnit of measure picklist: Each, License, Seat, Hour, Month, etc.
stock_keeping_unitSTRINGSKU from inventory/fulfillment system.
descriptionSTRINGProduct description.
ll_revenue_typeSTRINGListenLayer computed: recurring, one_time, or unknown. Set by matching this product against product_revenue_types rules in crm_revenue_config. Used to classify revenue on opportunities that include this product.
custom_fieldsJSONOrg-specific custom fields on products. Common in CPQ-heavy orgs: Billing_Type__c, Subscription_Term__c, Revenue_Recognition__c, etc.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. How this row was last written: cdc, bulk_import, rest_fetch, deletion_sweep.
_sync_batch_idSTRINGInternal. Which batch/job produced the last write.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.
_first_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When we first ingested this record. Never overwritten.
_cdc_replay_idSTRINGInternal. Last Pub/Sub replay ID. NULL for non-CDC sources.

⬡crm_account_revenue_monthly

Monthly revenue snapshots per CRM account. One row per account per month. Contains ARR/MRR at month end, revenue movement (expansion/contraction/churn), cumulative LTV, customer status, and contract renewal dates. Computed by revenue processor using crm_revenue_config rules. config_version enables recomputation on config change. Used for: revenue trends, cohort analysis, net retention rate, churn timing, LTV by cohort.

Partition: monthCluster: account_id, source_org_id, crm_account_record_id
FieldTypeDescription
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLCRM system: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLCRM instance ID. SF org ID, HubSpot portal ID, etc.
crm_account_record_idSTRING NOT NULLFK → crm_account.record_id. The CRM account this snapshot is for.
monthDATE NOT NULLFirst day of the month this snapshot represents (e.g., 2026-05-01). Partition key.
arrFLOAT64Annual recurring revenue as of this month, in corporate currency. From crm_account.ll_revenue.current_arr at snapshot time.
mrrFLOAT64Monthly recurring revenue in corporate currency (arr / 12).
one_time_revenueFLOAT64Non-recurring revenue recognized this month in corporate currency.
total_revenue_this_monthFLOAT64Total revenue recognized this month in corporate currency (mrr + one_time_revenue).
arr_changeFLOAT64ARR delta from prior month in corporate currency. Positive = expansion, negative = contraction. Zero = flat.
movement_typeSTRINGRevenue movement classification: new (first revenue ever), expansion (ARR increased), contraction (ARR decreased but > 0), churn (ARR went to 0), reactivation (ARR back from 0), flat (no change).
ltv_to_dateFLOAT64Cumulative lifetime value in corporate currency through this month.
months_as_customerINT64Number of months since first revenue (first_revenue_at on crm_account). Defines cohort age.
is_customerBOOLWhether this account was a customer this month (ARR > 0 or had revenue activity).
is_churnedBOOLWhether this account churned THIS month (was customer last month, ARR went to 0 this month).
active_contractsINT64Number of active contracts this month.
next_contract_end_dateDATEEarliest ending contract date. Renewal risk indicator — closer dates mean higher urgency.
corporate_currencySTRINGCorporate/base currency (ISO 4217) of all monetary values in this row. All values are pre-converted to corporate currency at the opportunity level — no conversion happens at snapshot time.
computed_atTIMESTAMPWhen this snapshot was computed or last updated.
config_versionSTRINGHash of crm_revenue_config used to compute this snapshot. When the user changes their revenue config, rows with a stale config_version are recomputed.

⬡crm_pipeline_daily

Daily pipeline snapshots. Captures deal counts, pipeline value (using ll_revenue from crm_revenue_config), weighted pipeline (platform and AI probabilities), forecast category breakdown, velocity metrics, and AI intelligence. Same revenue config drives both pipeline and LTV/churn. Historical snapshots preserved on config change. Used for: pipeline trends, forecasting, coverage analysis, velocity tracking.

Partition: snapshot_dateCluster: account_id, source_org_id, pipeline_name
FieldTypeDescription
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLCRM system: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLCRM instance ID.
pipeline_nameSTRING NOT NULLWhich pipeline this snapshot is for. SF: derived from RecordType or Sales Process. HubSpot/Pipedrive: explicit pipeline name. "default" if the org has only one pipeline.
snapshot_dateDATE NOT NULLThe date this snapshot represents. Partition key. One snapshot per pipeline per day.
open_dealsINT64Total number of open opportunities in this pipeline.
deals_by_stageJSONDeal count per stage as JSON object. E.g., {"Qualification": 12, "Proposal": 8, "Negotiation": 3}. Keys are stage_name values.
total_pipeline_arrFLOAT64Sum of ll_revenue.arr_corporate for all open deals. Corporate currency. Total annualized recurring pipeline value.
total_pipeline_one_timeFLOAT64Sum of ll_revenue.one_time_corporate for all open deals. Corporate currency.
weighted_pipelineFLOAT64Sum of (ll_revenue.arr_corporate * probability / 100) for all open deals. Uses CRM platform probability. Corporate currency.
ai_weighted_pipelineFLOAT64Sum of (ll_revenue.arr_corporate * ll_pipeline.ai_probability) for all open deals. AI-corrected probability. Corporate currency. More predictive than platform-weighted.
value_by_stageJSONPipeline ARR (corporate currency) per stage as JSON object. E.g., {"Qualification": 450000, "Proposal": 320000}. Keys are stage_name values.
corporate_currencySTRINGCorporate/base currency (ISO 4217) of all monetary values. All values summed from opportunity ll_revenue._corporate fields.
commit_valueFLOAT64Sum of ll_revenue.arr_corporate where forecast_category_name = Commit.
best_case_valueFLOAT64Sum of ll_revenue.arr_corporate where forecast_category_name = Best Case.
most_likely_valueFLOAT64Sum of ll_revenue.arr_corporate where forecast_category_name = Most Likely.
pipeline_category_valueFLOAT64Sum of ll_revenue.arr_corporate where forecast_category_name = Pipeline.
omitted_valueFLOAT64Sum of ll_revenue.arr_corporate where forecast_category_name = Omitted.
avg_deal_age_daysFLOAT64Average days since created_date for open deals. High values indicate aging pipeline.
avg_stage_age_daysFLOAT64Average days in current stage for open deals. High values indicate stalled pipeline.
stalled_dealsINT64Count of OPEN deals (is_closed = FALSE) where ll_pipeline.is_stalled = TRUE. Closed deals are never counted as stalled.
deals_created_todayINT64New opportunities created on this snapshot_date. Measures pipeline generation.
pipeline_created_arrFLOAT64ll_revenue.arr_corporate of opportunities created on this snapshot_date. Corporate currency.
deals_won_todayINT64Opportunities that moved to Closed Won on this date.
won_arr_todayFLOAT64ll_revenue.arr_corporate of opportunities won on this date. Corporate currency.
deals_lost_todayINT64Opportunities that moved to Closed Lost on this date.
lost_arr_todayFLOAT64ll_revenue.arr_corporate of opportunities lost on this date. Corporate currency.
period_targetFLOAT64Revenue target for the current period in corporate currency. From account_settings or user-set. NULL if no target configured.
coverage_ratioFLOAT64total_pipeline_arr / period_target. 3.0 = 3x coverage. NULL if no target. Industry standard: 3-4x for healthy pipeline.
intelligence.pipeline_healthSTRINGAI-assessed pipeline health: healthy, thin (not enough deals), bloated (too many low-quality deals), stalled (deals not progressing), top_heavy (too concentrated in late stages).
intelligence.top_riskSTRINGSingle most important pipeline risk. E.g., "3 deals stalled in Negotiation >30 days totaling $280K ARR", "No new pipeline created in 2 weeks", "80% of pipeline closes this month — thin next month".
intelligence.recommendationsJSONJSON array of actionable recommendations. E.g., ["Follow up on 3 stalled Negotiation deals", "Pipeline coverage for Q3 is 1.8x — need $400K more"].
intelligence.summarySTRINGAI-generated plain-English pipeline assessment for this day.
intelligence.confidenceFLOAT64AI confidence in the assessment (0.0-1.0).
intelligence.last_analyzed_atTIMESTAMPWhen the AI last analyzed this pipeline snapshot.
config_versionSTRINGHash of crm_revenue_config used to compute values in this snapshot. Historical snapshots are preserved as-is. Customer can request recompute of historical snapshots after config change.
computed_atTIMESTAMPWhen this snapshot was computed.

⬡crm_changelog

Typed CRM change stream — append-only history of every field change on every CRM record. Six sources: live CDC, bulk import synthetic, historical backfill, REST recovery, non-CDC polling detection, computed attribute transitions. Uses our normalized field names (not CRM-native). Source: CRM Ingest Processor. Used for: trigger rule evaluation, attribution, audit trail, historical analysis.

Partition: DATE(event_timestamp)Cluster: account_id, source_org_id, object_type, record_id
FieldTypeDescription
event_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique event ID. Generated by our pipeline. Not from the CRM.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLCRM instance/org ID.
record_idSTRING NOT NULLThe CRM record this change occurred on.
object_typeSTRING NOT NULLCRM object type: Lead, Contact, Account, Opportunity, Case, Task, Event, Campaign, Quote, or any custom object API name.
change_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhat happened: create, update, delete, undelete.
field_nameSTRINGWhich field changed. Uses OUR snake_case column name (not CRM native). Custom fields prefixed: custom.ARR__c. ll_* for computed attribute transitions. NULL for delete/undelete (record-level events).
old_valueSTRINGPrevious value as string. NULL for create events and delete events.
new_valueSTRINGNew value as string. NULL for delete events.
currency_iso_codeSTRINGISO 4217 currency code. Populated ONLY when field_name is a currency field (amount, annual_revenue, etc.). NULL for non-currency fields and single-currency orgs.
actor_user_idSTRINGCRM user who made the change. NULL for system changes, synthetic events, and computed attribute transitions.
modstampTIMESTAMPWhen the CRM recorded the change (SystemModstamp). For live CDC, this is real-time. For bulk imports, the records SystemModstamp at import time. For polling-detected changes, the records LastModifiedDate.
event_timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLThe meaningful business time. For most events, equals modstamp. For historical backfill: the HISTORICAL moment (e.g., when Opp actually moved to Closed Won from OpportunityHistory). For computed attributes: when the transition was detected. Trigger rules use this for attribution.
raw_event_idSTRINGFK → crm_changelog_raw.event_id. Traces this typed entry back to the raw payload that produced it. NULL for computed attribute transitions (no raw payload).
sync_batch_idSTRINGWhich CDC batch, Bulk API job, polling run, or synthesis run produced this row.
is_syntheticBOOL NOT NULLFALSE for live CDC events, REST recovery, and polling-detected changes (these represent real CRM changes). TRUE for events we generate: bulk_import, field_history, legacy_history, record_field, computed_attribute.
synthetic_sourceSTRINGNULL for live/real events. For synthetic: bulk_import (initial load), field_history (from OpportunityFieldHistory etc.), legacy_history (from OpportunityHistory), record_field (from static timestamp field like CloseDate), computed_attribute (ll_* field transition).
synthetic_rule_idSTRINGWhich trigger rule requested the backfill synthesis. NULL for bulk_import and computed_attribute sources. Used for clean regeneration: DELETE WHERE synthetic_rule_id = X.
synthetic_generated_atTIMESTAMPWhen WE generated this synthetic event. Different from event_timestamp which is the historical business moment. NULL for non-synthetic events.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. How this entry was produced: cdc (live Pub/Sub), bulk_import (initial load), rest_fetch (GAP_OVERFLOW/gap recovery), deletion_sweep (daily reconciliation), bulk_polling (non-CDC object change detection), computed_attribute (ll_* transition), backfill_synthesis (historical backfill).
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline wrote this row.

⬡crm_changelog_raw

Raw CRM change payloads — append-only archive of every payload that entered the system. CDC event envelopes, bulk import records, REST responses, synthesized backfill events. Stored as JSON. Never modified. Source: all CRM ingest paths. Used for: debugging, reprocessing, audit trail.

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: account_id, object_type, record_id
FieldTypeDescription
event_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique event identifier. Generated by our pipeline.
event_typeSTRING NOT NULLCRM event type: record_created, record_updated, field_changed, record_deleted, bulk_import, backfill_synthesized.
event_sourceSTRINGEvent source: always crm for this table.
timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this event occurred.
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRINGCRM instance/org identifier.
object_typeSTRINGCRM object: Lead, Contact, Account, Opportunity, Case, Task, etc.
record_idSTRINGCRM record ID.
change_typeSTRINGCREATE, UPDATE, DELETE, UNDELETE.
is_syntheticBOOLTRUE for events we generate (bulk_import, backfill). FALSE for live CDC/REST.
synthetic_sourceSTRINGNULL for live. Values: bulk_import, field_history, legacy_history, record_field.
payloadJSONFull event payload — CDC change data, REST response, bulk record snapshot, or synthesized backfill event.
received_atTIMESTAMPWhen our pipeline received this payload.
processed_atTIMESTAMPWhen processing completed.

⬡crm_record_persons

CRM record to person junction — materialized result of identity resolution and attribution walks. Two-hop model: walk stores the intermediate Lead/Contact/Account (Hop 1), person_id is the last mile from identity resolution (Hop 2, may be NULL if pending). Every CRM object with configured walks gets person links here. Source: CRM Ingest Processor (direct) + Walk Evaluator (walked). Used for: attribution queries connecting CRM records to web sessions via persons.

Partition: DATE(first_associated_at)Cluster: account_id, person_id, crm_object_type, crm_record_id
FieldTypeDescription
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM system: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, etc.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLCRM instance/org ID.
crm_object_typeSTRING NOT NULLSource object: Opportunity, Case, Campaign, Quote, Task, Event, or any custom object. For direct entries: Lead, Contact.
crm_record_idSTRING NOT NULLSource CRM record ID. FK to the corresponding crm_* table.
resolved_to_objectSTRING NOT NULLThe CRM object type the walk landed on: Contact, Lead, or Account. For direct entries (Contact→Person), this equals crm_object_type. For walked entries (Opportunity→Contact via ContactRole), this is "Contact". This is Hop 1 of the two-hop model.
resolved_to_record_idSTRING NOT NULLThe specific CRM record ID the walk landed on. For direct: same as crm_record_id. For walked: the intermediate Contact/Lead/Account record ID. FK to the corresponding crm_* table.
person_idSTRINGFK → persons.person_id. The ListenLayer person. NULL if the resolved_to object has not yet been matched to a person (identity resolution pending). At query time, NULL entries can be resolved by JOINing to direct entries for the same resolved_to_record_id.
relationshipSTRINGType of relationship: primary, related, owner (often excluded from attribution — seller not buyer), creator.
resolved_viaSTRINGHow this link was established. Direct: email_hash_match, phone_hash_match, manual. Walked: walk:direct_persons, walk:parent_account, walk:lateral_contacts, walk:<custom_leg_name>.
walk_leg_nameSTRINGWhich walk leg produced this entry. NULL for direct resolutions. E.g., "direct_persons", "parent_account", "lateral_contacts".
confidenceFLOAT64Confidence (0-1). Direct email matches = 1.0. Walked = may be lower.
first_associated_atTIMESTAMPWhen the CRM record first became associated with this person/intermediate object. CRITICAL for session pruning: session_start_ts >= first_associated_at.
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen this junction row was created.
last_verified_atTIMESTAMPWhen last verified by walk evaluator or identity resolver. Stale = possibly broken.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. identity_resolver (direct), walk_evaluator (walked), manual.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When this row was last written.

⬡crm_record_companies

CRM record to company junction — materialized result of identity resolution and attribution walks. Two-hop model: walk stores the intermediate Account (Hop 1), company_id is the last mile from domain matching (Hop 2, may be NULL). Source: CRM Ingest Processor (direct) + Walk Evaluator (walked). Used for: attribution queries connecting CRM records to web sessions via companies.

Partition: DATE(first_associated_at)Cluster: account_id, company_id, crm_object_type, crm_record_id

relationshipSTRINGType: primary (this record IS the company — Account), parent_account (linked via Account FK), related.

walk_leg_nameSTRINGWhich walk leg. NULL for direct.
confidenceFLOAT64Confidence (0-1). Direct domain matches = 1.0.
first_associated_atTIMESTAMPWhen the CRM record first became associated with this company/intermediate Account. CRITICAL for session pruning.
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen this junction row was created.
last_verified_atTIMESTAMPWhen last verified.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. identity_resolver, walk_evaluator, manual.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When this row was last written.

⬡crm_custom_objects

Generic table for ALL custom CRM objects — catch-all for objects without typed tables (custom objects like Subscription__c, managed packages, standard objects not given typed tables). All fields in JSON. JOIN to crm_object_registry for AI-generated object description and metadata. Source: CRM via CDC or bulk import. Used for: flexible custom object storage, trigger evaluation via JSON fields.

Partition: DATE(last_modified_date)Cluster: object_api_name, account_id, source_org_id
FieldTypeDescription

last_modified_dateTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen last modified in the source CRM.
system_modstampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLSystem timestamp for sync watermarking and last-modstamp-wins.

JOIN to crm_object_registry on (account_id, crm_type, source_org_id, object_api_name) for object metadata and AI description.
object_labelSTRINGHuman-readable label from CRM metadata. Denormalized from crm_object_registry for convenience.

record_type_idSTRINGRecord type ID if the custom object uses Record Types.

last_modified_by_idSTRINGWho last modified the record.

Query via JSON_VALUE(fields, "$.status"). This is the primary data store — no typed columns exist for custom object fields.
relationship_fieldsJSONExtracted relationship/lookup fields only. E.g., {"account__c": "001XYZ", "contact__c": "003ABC", "opportunity__c": "006DEF"}. Subset of fields JSON — only fields marked is_relationship=true in crm_object_registry.fields. Walk evaluator reads this instead of scanning the full fields blob.
_sync_sourceSTRINGInternal. How this row was last written: cdc, bulk_import, rest_fetch, deletion_sweep.
_sync_batch_idSTRINGInternal. Which batch/job produced the last write.
_synced_atTIMESTAMPInternal. When our pipeline last wrote this row.

⬡crm_object_registry

CRM object registry — internal control plane. One row per CRM object per customer. Tracks schema fingerprint, field inventory, CDC/sync status, bulk import progress, identity resolution config, attribution walk config, trigger defaults, and AI-generated object descriptions. Source: populated by schema discovery on CRM connect, refreshed daily. Used for: driving all CRM sync, walk evaluation, and intelligence processing.

Partition: noneCluster: account_id, crm_type, source_org_id
FieldTypeDescription
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID.
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLWhich CRM: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive, zoho.
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLCRM instance/org ID.
object_api_nameSTRING NOT NULLCRM object API name: Opportunity, Lead, Contact, Account, Subscription__c, etc. This is the source-native name.
object_labelSTRINGHuman-readable label from CRM metadata (e.g., "Subscription" for Subscription__c).
is_standard_objectBOOLTRUE for standard CRM objects (Opportunity, Lead, etc.). FALSE for custom objects (__c suffix in SF). Determines whether data goes in a typed table (crm_opportunity) or crm_custom_objects.
table_nameSTRINGWhich BQ table holds this objects data: crm_opportunity, crm_lead, crm_custom_objects, etc. For standard objects, the typed table name. For custom objects, always crm_custom_objects.
is_activeBOOLWhether we are actively syncing this object. FALSE if customer disabled it or object was deprecated.
ai_descriptionSTRINGAI-generated natural language description of this objects purpose. For standard objects: predefined ("Tracks sales deals and pipeline revenue"). For custom objects: AI-analyzed from field names, relationships, and sample data ("Tracks recurring subscription contracts with renewal dates, MRR values, and billing cadence"). Surfaces in dashboard, used by AI intelligence layer for context.
ai_description_confidenceFLOAT64Confidence in the AI description (0-1). Higher for standard objects. Lower for custom objects with ambiguous schemas.
ai_description_generated_atTIMESTAMPWhen the AI description was last generated.
schema_fingerprintSTRINGHash of the full field set. Rotates on ANY schema change (additions, renames, type changes, deprecations). Daily Metadata API diff compares against this to detect drift.
fieldsJSONFull field inventory as JSON array. Each entry: {api_name, label, type, is_required, is_relationship, references_object, our_column_name (for standard objects), is_custom}. Drives field mapping and schema validation.

sync_methodSTRINGPrimary sync method: cdc (real-time Pub/Sub), bulk_polling (scheduled Bulk API), metadata_api (config tables), rest_polling (per-object REST fallback).
sync_polling_cadence_minutesINT64For non-CDC objects, how often we poll in minutes. Default: 15 for attribution-critical, 60 for others. NULL for CDC objects.
cdc_statusSTRINGCDC state: enabled, unsupported, failed, pending, not_applicable. Not_applicable for metadata/config objects.
cdc_enabled_atTIMESTAMPWhen CDC was programmatically enabled for this object.
last_cdc_replay_idSTRINGLast Pub/Sub replay ID processed. For resuming CDC subscription after disconnect.
last_synced_atTIMESTAMPWhen the last successful sync completed (any method).
bulk_import_statusSTRINGImport state: pending, in_progress, paused, completed, failed, partial.
