bulk_import_last_modstampTIMESTAMPHighest SystemModstamp imported. For resume.
bulk_import_failure_reasonSTRINGPopulated when status=failed.
bulk_import_paced_scheduleJSONDay-by-day pacing plan when auto-pacing is active. JSON: [{day, target_records, actual_records, status}].
field_history_tracking_enabledBOOLWhether CRM has field-level history tracking enabled for this object.
field_history_tracked_fieldsJSONArray of field API names being tracked. E.g., ["StageName", "Amount", "CloseDate"].
field_history_table_nameSTRINGCRM table name for field history. E.g., "OpportunityFieldHistory", "LeadHistory".
legacy_history_table_availableBOOLWhether a legacy history table exists. Always TRUE for SF Opportunity (OpportunityHistory).
legacy_history_table_nameSTRINGCRM table name. E.g., "OpportunityHistory".
field_history_max_lookback_monthsINT64Max lookback for field history. SF hard limit: 18 months.
identity_resolutionJSONConfig for feeding persons_raw and companies_raw from this object. JSON: {feeds_persons, person_email_field, person_phone_field, person_name_fields, person_confidence, feeds_companies, company_domain_source, company_domain_field, company_name_field}. Defaults seeded from object-defaults.yaml; customer-overridable.
attribution_walksJSONArray of walk leg configs for the attribution walk evaluator. Each: {leg_name, leg_type (direct_personparent_recordlateral_expansion), enabled, person_relationship_filter, parent_object, parent_fk_field, parent_resolution, expand_to_object, expand_to_fk_field, expand_resolution}. Default walks per standard object from object-defaults.yaml; customer-overridable. Custom objects: empty until customer configures.
trigger_defaultsJSONDefault trigger evaluation config: {use_event_timestamp_field (e.g., "CloseDate" for Opportunity, "CreatedDate" default), fallback_to_modstamp (bool)}. Determines which timestamp to use for attribution when a trigger fires on this object.
overridden_by_customerBOOLTRUE if customer has customized any config on this object (attribution walks, identity resolution, trigger defaults). When TRUE, global default updates will NOT overwrite this row.
overridden_atTIMESTAMPWhen overrides were last applied.
defaults_versionSTRINGWhich version of object-defaults.yaml was used to seed this row. Used to determine if re-seeding is needed when defaults are updated (only for non-overridden rows).
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen this registry row was created (first schema discovery).
updated_atTIMESTAMPWhen this registry row was last modified.

⬡account_settings

Per-customer account configuration. One row per account. Contains timezone, business context (for AI prompts), enrichment settings, ICP definition, client domains, competitor domains. CRM integration config: connected sources, computed rules, API budgets, trigger config. Source: Supabase → settings publisher. Used for: all processing that needs account-level configuration.

Partition: noneCluster: none
FieldTypeDescription
account_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique account identifier
timezoneSTRING NOT NULLIANA timezone (e.g. America/Denver)
sdk_versionSTRINGCurrent SDK version
plan_tierSTRINGAccount plan tier
business_nameSTRINGCompany or brand name
business_contextSTRINGDescription of the business, industry, target audience
website_descriptionSTRINGWhat the website is for, main purpose
industrySTRINGIndustry vertical (SaaS, ecommerce, healthcare, etc.)
enrichment_configJSONEnrichment settings controlling whether GCP identity-resolver calls enrichment APIs. Contains b2b and b2c sub-objects with enabled, limit_reached, monthly_limit, monthly_used, auto_enrich fields.
client_domainsJSONClient own domains for internal labeling. Companies and persons matching these domains are labeled is_internal = TRUE. Example: ["listenlayer.com", "screen1.io"]
icp_descriptionSTRINGFree-text ideal customer profile description, customer-written. Used by LLM for competitor classification and future ICP scoring.
known_competitor_domainsJSONKnown competitor domains, explicit overrides auto-classified without LLM. Example: ["hotjar.com", "fullstory.com", "heap.io"]
icp_definitionJSONStructured ICP scoring criteria for future use. Contains criteria array with field/operator/values/weight, role_categories, and scoring thresholds.
connected_sourcesJSONConnected CRM sources — which CRM instances are linked to this account. Each entry contains crm_type, source_org_id, instance_url, status, and sync config.
crm_computed_rulesJSONCRM computed attribute rules — deterministic rules that set ll_* fields on CRM records. AI-generated on CRM connect; customer approves/edits in dashboard. Evaluated inline on every CDC/bulk write by Processor 1.
sf_api_budgetsJSONSalesforce API budget configuration. Controls daily API call caps, Pub/Sub event caps, alert thresholds, and bulk import settings. Per-org overrides possible via connected_sources.
trigger_configJSONCRM trigger configuration defaults. Controls replay window, auto-approve settings, and confidence thresholds.
crm_revenue_configJSONCRM revenue normalization config — two-layer AI opinion model. Layer 1 (field_opinions): per-field classification of revenue fields on opportunities. Layer 2 (rollup): how field opinions combine into ARR, one-time, and LTV formulas. AI-generated on CRM connect; customer reviews and overrides per-field or at rollup level.
connector_secretsJSONPointers to Secret Manager entries for CRM connector tokens. Never contains the tokens themselves.
created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLRow creation timestamp
updated_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLRow last-modified timestamp

⬡active_accounts

Registry of all customer accounts and their corresponding BigQuery dataset names. Used by the relationship scanner and any scheduled process that needs to iterate over accounts — replaces expensive INFORMATION_SCHEMA queries. Written by the account provisioning service when a new account is created; status set to inactive on deactivation. Dataset: ll_platform (shared platform dataset, not per-customer).

Partition: none (tiny table — hundreds of rows maximum)Cluster: none

statusSTRINGCurrent account status: active, inactive, suspended. Default active
plan_tierSTRINGAccount plan tier used for feature gating, e.g. starter, growth, enterprise
created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this account row was first created

⬡ads_jobs

Ads background job tracking per platform. One row per job execution. Tracks all ads background jobs: daily platform syncs, historical backfills, budget summary updates, creative asset downloads, and intelligence runs. Each platform connector (Google, Meta, LinkedIn, Microsoft, TikTok) writes its own jobs here. Simpler than crm_jobs because ads jobs are more uniform — almost all are daily scheduled syncs with a target_date, each platform is independent, and jobs are shorter (minutes, not hours). Dataset: observability (centralized, NOT per-customer).

Partition: DATE(created_at)Cluster: account_id, platform, job_type, status

target_dateDATEThe date being synced. NULL for non-date jobs such as creative_asset_download and intelligence_run
statusSTRING NOT NULLCurrent job status: pending, running, completed, failed, partial, abandoned
steps_completedARRAY<STRING>Steps that finished successfully in this run: dimensions, metrics, clicks, search_queries, conversion_actions, creative_assets, budget_summary, intelligence
steps_failedARRAY<STRING>Steps that failed in this run
last_errorSTRINGMost recent error message. NULL when status is completed
records_processedINT64Total records written across all steps (campaigns + metrics + clicks + etc.)

run_idSTRINGUUID of the current or most recent run. Correlates with traces and logs
duration_msINT64Total job duration in milliseconds for the last run

created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this job was first created. Partition key
updated_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this job row was last modified

⬡cost_registry

Universal cost registry — versioned pricing for all vendors, models, APIs, and compute resources. Supports LLM models (input_tokens, output_tokens, cached_tokens per model), external APIs (per api_call or per match), and compute (per cpu_ms, request, events). Versioned by effective_from/effective_to dates so historical costs are always computed using the price in effect at the time of the usage event. When a vendor changes pricing, a new row is added with the new effective_from and effective_to is set on the old row. Joined with usage_ledger to compute actual costs. Dataset: observability (cross-account).

Partition: noneCluster: none
FieldTypeDescription
vendorSTRING NOT NULLVendor identifier: xai, kickfire, atdata, telnyx, abstract, capi, mixrank, google, cloudflare, gcp, internal
itemSTRING NOT NULLSpecific model, API product, or compute resource being priced, e.g. grok-4-1-fast-non-reasoning, kickfire_ip_lookup, cpu_ms
unit_typeSTRING NOT NULLUnit of measure for cost_per_unit: input_tokens, output_tokens, cached_tokens, api_call, match, cpu_ms, request, events
cost_per_unitFLOAT64 NOT NULLCost in USD per one unit of unit_type
currencySTRINGCurrency of cost_per_unit. Default USD
effective_fromDATE NOT NULLDate when this price took effect
effective_toDATEDate when this price was superseded. NULL means this is the current active price
notesSTRINGFree-text notes about this price entry, e.g. reason for change or contract details
updated_atTIMESTAMPWhen this row was last updated. Defaults to CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()

⬡crm_jobs

CRM background job tracking. One row per job execution. Tracks all CRM background jobs: bulk imports, CDC recovery, REST polling, revenue snapshots, pipeline snapshots, schema discovery, and rule generation. More complex than ads_jobs because CRM jobs vary significantly — bulk imports run for hours with record-level cursors, CDC recovery operates on replay IDs, and revenue/pipeline snapshots are account-wide rather than date-scoped. Multiple concurrent jobs per account are supported (different objects importing simultaneously). Dataset: observability (centralized, NOT per-customer).

Partition: DATE(created_at)Cluster: account_id, job_type, status
FieldTypeDescription
job_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique job UUID. Generated on job creation
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID
crm_typeSTRING NOT NULLCRM system: salesforce, hubspot, dynamics, pipedrive
source_org_idSTRING NOT NULLCRM instance identifier: SF org ID, HubSpot portal ID, Dynamics org GUID, etc. Together with crm_type uniquely identifies the connected CRM instance
job_typeSTRING NOT NULLJob type: bulk_import, cdc_recovery, rest_polling, revenue_snapshot, pipeline_snapshot, schema_discovery, rule_generation
object_typeSTRINGCRM object being processed, e.g. Opportunity, Contact, Account. NULL for account-wide jobs such as revenue_snapshot and pipeline_snapshot
statusSTRING NOT NULLCurrent job status: pending, running, completed, failed, partial, abandoned, cancelled
last_errorSTRINGMost recent error message. NULL when status is completed
records_totalINT64Total records to process. NULL when count is not known upfront (e.g. CDC recovery). Set on job start for bulk imports
records_processedINT64Records successfully processed so far in this job
records_failedINT64Records that failed processing. Subtract from records_processed to get net successful records
retry_countINT64 NOT NULLNumber of retry attempts so far. Starts at 0 on first run
max_retriesINT64 NOT NULLMaximum retry attempts before the job is abandoned. Default 5 for short jobs, 10 for bulk imports
next_retry_afterTIMESTAMPEarliest timestamp at which a retry is allowed. Exponential backoff schedule: 1m, 5m, 30m, 2h, 12h. NULL if not scheduled for retry
checkpointJSONJob-specific checkpoint for resuming after failure. Shape varies by job_type — bulk_import: {last_record_id, bulk_job_id, page_cursor}; rest_polling: {watermark_timestamp, last_modified_date}; cdc_recovery: {replay_id, gap_start, gap_end}; revenue_snapshot: {last_account_processed, config_version}; pipeline_snapshot: {last_pipeline_processed, snapshot_date}
triggerSTRINGWhat started this job: scheduled (cron), on_connect (first sync after CRM connection), manual (user-initiated), cdc_gap_detected (gap in CDC stream triggered recovery), config_change (revenue config updated requiring recompute)
run_idSTRINGUUID of the current or most recent run. Correlates with event_traces and logs for debugging
duration_msINT64Total job duration in milliseconds. Updated on completion or failure
created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this job was first created. Partition key
updated_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this job row was last modified

⬡dlq_failures

Multi-source Dead Letter Queue (DLQ) landings. One row per message that exceeded max_delivery_attempts on its source Pub/Sub subscription. Permanent record of messages that failed processing across all pipeline services: collect-processor, event-processor, backend-settings, web-settings. Identity fields (event_id, account_id, etc.) are best-effort — extracted from Pub/Sub message attributes set by the producer, not the failed payload itself, so they survive even when the payload is malformed. Distinct from event_traces (3-day retention, per-stage lifecycle) — this table preserves the full payload for post-mortem analysis. Dataset: observability (cross-account).

Partition: DATE(dlq_landed_at)Cluster: account_id, event_type, rejection_reason
FieldTypeDescription
event_idSTRINGUnique event identifier extracted from Pub/Sub message attributes. Best-effort — may be NULL if the producer did not set it or the message was malformed
account_idSTRINGListenLayer account ID extracted from message attributes. Best-effort
event_typeSTRINGEvent type extracted from message attributes. Best-effort
parent_event_idSTRINGParent event ID extracted from message attributes. Best-effort
device_idSTRINGDevice ID extracted from message attributes. Best-effort
correlation_idSTRINGCorrelation ID extracted from message attributes for tracing across services. Best-effort
message_idSTRING NOT NULLPub/Sub messageId from the source topic. Unique identifier for the Pub/Sub message itself
publish_timeTIMESTAMPWhen the producer originally published this message to the source topic
dlq_landed_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this message arrived on the DLQ topic. Partition key
source_subscriptionSTRINGThe DLQ subscription that consumed this message. Acts as the dispatch key identifying which source service produced the failure
delivery_attemptINT64The delivery attempt number when the message gave up and was routed to the DLQ
rejection_reasonSTRINGFailure classification: validation_failed (Zod or schema rejection), parse_failed:* (undecodable payload — corrupt base64 or JSON), processing_failed (downstream operation threw — R2 publish failed, BigQuery insert failed, gateway unreachable)
failure_messageSTRINGTop-level error string describing what went wrong
failure_pathsARRAY<STRING>Structured field paths identifying where validation failed, e.g. Zod field paths. Empty array for processing_failed where there are no structured paths
failure_messagesARRAY<STRING>Per-path error messages corresponding to each entry in failure_paths. Empty for processing_failed
attributesJSONFull set of Pub/Sub message attributes as set by the producer: eventType, accountId, gateway, environment, correlationId, receivedAt, schemaVersion, intent. These survive even when the payload itself is malformed
payloadJSONFull rejected payload decoded from the Pub/Sub message data field. Truncated for very large payloads — check payload_truncated
payload_truncatedBOOLTRUE if the payload was truncated before storage due to size limits
payload_size_bytesINT64Size of the original payload in bytes before any truncation

⬡edge_cache_traces

Edge cache, AI memoization, and circuit-breaker operational metrics. Sampled (1% default) rows emitted by the Cloudflare edge worker as console.log lines and ingested via Logpush. Three distinct trace kinds share this table — discriminate by the kind column before interpreting other fields. Intentionally separate from event_traces: event_traces is keyed on event_id for per-event lifecycle reconstruction; this table is keyed on (kind, loader, account_id, timestamp) for hit-rate, cost-avoided, and stability analytics. Sampling math means aggregates require COUNT(*)/sampling_rate extrapolation. Dataset: observability (cross-account).

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: kind, loader, account_id
FieldTypeDescription
timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLLogpush envelope timestamp derived from TIMESTAMP_MILLIS(EventTimestampMs). Partition key
kindSTRING NOT NULLTrace type discriminator: cache_load (cached-loader.ts L1/L2/L3 result and hash validation), cache_ai (cachedAI() KV memoization for Workers AI model calls), circuit_transition (queue.ts circuit-breaker state transitions — NOT sampled, emitted unconditionally because rare and alerting-load-bearing)
account_idSTRINGAccount scope for this trace. NULL for global signals such as circuit_transition rows which are not account-scoped
loaderSTRINGMeaning varies by kind. cache_load: the loader name — settings, consent, triggers, custom-vars, reveal-settings, page-class, page-content, page-class-hash. cache_ai: the caller name string identifying which code path invoked cachedAI(). circuit_transition: NULL
sourceSTRINGMeaning varies by kind. cache_load: result source — l1 (hot cache hit), l1-stale-fallback (served stale from L1), l2-kv (KV cache hit), l3-r2 (R2 object store hit), not-found (miss), kv (page-class KV hit), kv-bypass (forced miss, preview/debug), kv-empty (KV had no entry), hash-throttle (rate-limited hash check), hash-changed (content changed, cache invalidated), hash-unchanged (content confirmed unchanged). cache_ai: hit (served from KV cache), miss (model was called), bypass (forced cache bypass), kv-error (KV read failed), corrupt (cached payload was invalid). circuit_transition: NULL
latency_msINT64Wall-clock time in milliseconds of the cache GET or model call — whichever path was actually taken
hash_validatedBOOLcache_load only: TRUE if the L1 cache entry was confirmed valid against the KV hash before serving. NULL for cache_ai and circuit_transition rows
bypassBOOLcache_load only: TRUE if bypassL1Cache=true was set (preview or debug paths). These rows are excluded from hit-rate calculations. NULL for cache_ai and circuit_transition rows
modelSTRINGcache_ai only: AI model identifier, e.g. @cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct-fp8-fast. NULL for cache_load and circuit_transition rows
cachedBOOLcache_ai only: TRUE if the AI response was served from KV cache (avoiding a model invocation). FALSE if the model was actually called. NULL for cache_load and circuit_transition rows
breakerSTRINGcircuit_transition only: logical name of the circuit breaker that transitioned, e.g. gcp-forward. NULL for cache_load and cache_ai rows
from_stateSTRINGcircuit_transition only: breaker state before the transition — closed, open, or half-open. NULL for cache_load and cache_ai rows
to_stateSTRINGcircuit_transition only: breaker state after the transition — closed, open, or half-open. NULL for cache_load and cache_ai rows
failuresINT64circuit_transition only: consecutive failure count at the moment of transition. NULL for cache_load and cache_ai rows
opened_atTIMESTAMPcircuit_transition only: when the breaker last entered the open state. Used to compute open_duration_sec in the circuit_transitions view. NULL for cache_load and cache_ai rows
sampling_rateFLOAT64Sampling rate at emit time. 0.01 (1%) for cache_load and cache_ai rows; 1.0 for circuit_transition rows (always emitted). Multiply COUNT(*) by 1/sampling_rate to extrapolate to the full population

⬡edge_cache_traces_views

⬡event_traces

Pipeline event traces — tracks each event ID through every stage of the pipeline, from Cloudflare edge to BigQuery. One row per checkpoint — a single event produces multiple rows as it passes through stages. Used to answer "where did event X get stuck?" and "what is the drop rate per stage?". 3-day partition expiration — operational tracing table, not a permanent record. Distinct from edge_cache_traces (cache/circuit metrics) and dlq_failures (terminal DLQ archive). Dataset: observability (cross-account).

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: event_id, account_id, stage
FieldTypeDescription
event_idSTRINGUnique event identifier, e.g. evt_1776121552332_8axko8clm. NULL for batch operations that process many events at once
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID, e.g. 0c0d249e-cc38-4a75-a93b-cb6a8ee11e7a
event_typeSTRINGWire short code of the event type: pv (pageview), clk (click), pce (page content), pp (person detection), pc (company detection), etc.
parent_event_idSTRINGParent event ID when applicable, e.g. the pageview that spawned a form event
device_idSTRINGDevice ID for cross-stage correlation
stageSTRING NOT NULLPipeline checkpoint name identifying where in the pipeline this trace was recorded. Cloudflare Edge: edge_received, edge_processed, edge_forwarded, edge_queued, edge_queue_retry, edge_dlq. Collect Gateway: gateway_received, gateway_published. Collect Processor: processor_received, processor_validated, processor_rejection_imminent, processor_rejected_permanent, processor_bq_write, processor_gcs_write, processor_stage2. DLQ Consumer: processor_dlq_landed. Stage 2 Processors: stage2_received, stage2_processed, stage2_error. Pipeline Orchestrator: step_*, step_orphan_cleanup
statusSTRING NOT NULLOutcome at this stage: ok (completed successfully), error (failed — always includes error_message), warn (non-fatal anomaly), skipped (stage chose not to run), timeout (exceeded deadline), queued (deferred to retry queue), dlq (permanently failed, routed to dead-letter), partial (some but not all work completed), removed (row deleted by cleanup), deferred (action delayed, e.g. streaming-buffer block)
timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this checkpoint was recorded. Partition key
latency_msINT64Time spent at this stage in milliseconds
error_messageSTRINGError details when status is error or warn. NULL when status is ok
payload_bytesINT64Event payload size in bytes. Used to diagnose size-related drops or rejections
retry_countINT64Retry attempt number for this stage. 0 = first try
sourceSTRINGService that wrote this trace row: edge, gateway, processor, dlq-consumer, stage2_{name}, pipeline_orchestrator
metadataJSONStage-specific contextual details that do not fit the fixed columns. Examples: cf_colo (Cloudflare data centre), queue_name (CF retry queue), bq_table (BigQuery destination table), validation_errors (Zod error array)

⬡llm_traces

LLM call traces — full prompt and response for every LLM operation across all processors. One row per LLM API call. Linked to event_traces via event_id + stage for a complete picture of both the pipeline lifecycle and the AI work performed within it. Covers all LLM operations: person resolution, page classification, company classification, ICP scoring, intent classification, etc. 3-day partition expiration — same as event_traces. Dataset: observability (cross-account).

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: event_id, source
FieldTypeDescription
event_idSTRINGEvent identifier this LLM call was made for. NULL for batch operations such as pruning runs and ICP re-scoring that process many records without a single originating event
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID
call_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique UUID for this individual LLM API call. A single event may produce multiple call_id rows (e.g. one for resolution, one for classification)
stageSTRING NOT NULLPipeline stage where this LLM call occurred. Matches event_traces.stage — join on (event_id, stage) to correlate with the pipeline lifecycle trace
sourceSTRING NOT NULLService that made this LLM call. Matches event_traces.source — e.g. processor, stage2_identity, stage2_classification, pipeline_orchestrator
operationSTRINGHuman-readable name of the LLM operation being performed, e.g. person_resolution_full, page_classification, company_classification, icp_scoring, intent_classification, form_classification
modelSTRINGLLM model identifier used for this call, e.g. grok-4-fast, grok-4, gemini-2.5-flash
temperatureFLOAT64Sampling temperature parameter sent to the model
max_tokensINT64Maximum output tokens parameter sent to the model
system_promptSTRINGFull system prompt sent to the model for this call
user_promptSTRINGFull user prompt sent to the model for this call
input_tokensINT64Number of input (prompt) tokens consumed by this call
responseSTRINGFull LLM response text returned by the model
output_tokensINT64Number of output (completion) tokens produced by this call
finish_reasonSTRINGWhy the model stopped generating: stop (natural end), length (hit max_tokens limit), content_filter (blocked by safety filter)
validation_passedBOOLEANTRUE if the response passed structural validation (e.g. valid JSON matching expected schema). FALSE triggers a retry with a correction prompt
retry_countINT64Number of retry attempts for this call. 0 = first try. Increments when validation_passed is FALSE and the call is retried
correction_promptSTRINGCorrection prompt sent to the model on retry, describing what was wrong with the previous response. NULL on first attempts
latency_msINT64End-to-end latency of this LLM API call in milliseconds
timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this LLM call was made. Partition key
estimated_cost_usdFLOAT64Estimated cost of this call in USD, computed from input_tokens and output_tokens against cost_registry pricing

⬡pipeline_runs

Pipeline processing state per (account, pipeline, date). One mutable row per account per pipeline per date — updated in place as the pipeline progresses and retries. Tracks which dates have been processed for each account, enabling automatic retry of failed dates, backfill detection, processing visibility across all accounts, and version-based reprocessing. The primary control-plane table for the daily pipeline orchestrator. Dataset: observability (centralized, NOT per-customer).

Partition: target_dateCluster: account_id, pipeline, status
FieldTypeDescription
account_idSTRING NOT NULLAccount UUID identifying which customer's data this run processes
pipelineSTRING NOT NULLPipeline identifier: daily_transform, identity, ecommerce, raw_transform, raw_transform_retry, etc.
target_dateDATE NOT NULLThe calendar date being processed, e.g. 2026-04-27. Partition key
statusSTRING NOT NULLCurrent processing status: pending (queued but not started), running (currently executing), completed (all steps succeeded), failed (one or more steps errored, eligible for retry), abandoned (exceeded max_retries, needs manual intervention)
steps_completedARRAY<STRING>Steps that finished successfully in the most recent run
steps_failedARRAY<STRING>Steps that errored in the most recent run
last_errorSTRINGMost recent error message from the last failed step. NULL when status is completed
retry_countINT64 NOT NULLHow many times this (account, pipeline, target_date) combination has been attempted. Starts at 0
max_retriesINT64 NOT NULLMaximum retry attempts before the row is flipped to abandoned. Default 10
next_retry_afterTIMESTAMPEarliest timestamp at which a retry is allowed. Exponential backoff schedule: 15m, 1h, 6h, 24h. NULL when not scheduled for retry
triggerSTRINGWhat started the most recent run: scheduled (cron-triggered), manual (user or operator-triggered)
run_idSTRINGUUID of the current or most recent run. Correlates with event_traces and logs for debugging
duration_msINT64Total pipeline duration in milliseconds for the most recent run
processing_versionSTRINGGit SHA or Docker image tag of the pipeline code that produced the last run. Used to identify which code version processed a date and trigger version-based reprocessing after bug fixes
metadataJSONPipeline-specific control plane state that does not fit the fixed schema. Used by raw_transform to persist its watermark cursor: {last_watermark, window_start, window_end, rows_pending, rows_inserted, rows_failed, mode (batch or row_by_row), oldest_pending}. NULL when not set
created_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this row was first created
updated_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this row was last modified

⬡transform_failures

Record-level failure tracking across all transform pipelines. One row per failed record — mutable, updated in place on each retry attempt. Tracks bounded retry with exponential backoff, then permanent quarantine. The pending query for each pipeline LEFT JOINs against this table to exclude rows still in permanent quarantine or within their backoff window. On successful retry the row is deleted so retry_count does not compound across separate failure incidents. Two pipelines currently write here: raw_transform (main 5-min scheduled query) and raw_transform_retry (dedicated retry pass). Dataset: observability (centralized, NOT per-customer).

Partition: DATE(event_timestamp)Cluster: account_id, pipeline, event_id
FieldTypeDescription
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID of the account whose record failed
pipelineSTRING NOT NULLPipeline that produced the failure: raw_transform, crm_sync, app_transform, etc.
event_idSTRING NOT NULLThe failed record identifier. For raw_transform this is the web or app event_id
event_timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLOriginal event timestamp of the failed record. Used as the partition key for point-lookups into web_events_raw
error_messageSTRINGBigQuery or pipeline error message from the failed operation
retry_countINT64 NOT NULLHow many times this record has been attempted. Starts at 1 on first failure
max_retriesINT64Maximum retry attempts before permanent quarantine. Default 5. After this is exceeded is_permanent is set to TRUE
failure_classSTRINGClassification of the error derived from error_message via regex: transient (rate limits, 5xx, timeouts — retry on backoff), permanent (parse errors, schema errors — flip is_permanent=TRUE immediately), unknown (treat as transient until max_retries exhausted)
is_permanentBOOLTRUE when this row is excluded from retry forever. Set immediately on permanent failure_class, or after max_retries is exhausted for transient and unknown classes. Rows with is_permanent=TRUE must be cleared manually after a fix is deployed
next_retry_afterTIMESTAMPWhen this event becomes retry-eligible. Exponential backoff schedule by retry_count: 5m, 30m, 2h, 6h, then permanent. NULL means retry immediately (newly failed) or the row is permanent (check is_permanent)
first_failed_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this record first failed. Set once on INSERT, never updated
last_failed_atTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this record most recently failed. Updated on every retry attempt

⬡usage_ledger

Universal usage ledger — one row per cost-generating operation across all services on both Cloudflare and GCP. Records what was consumed (units, unit_type) and the actual cost when the service can compute it. LLM operations include token breakouts (input/output/cached) for precise per-model costing. API operations track per-call and per-match billing. Join to cost_registry on (vendor→service, item→operation, unit_type) with date range matching to compute costs when actual_cost_usd is not populated by the service. 90-day partition expiration covering billing cycles and dispute windows. Dataset: observability (cross-account).

Partition: DATE(timestamp)Cluster: account_id, category, service
FieldTypeDescription
account_idSTRING NOT NULLListenLayer account ID being served by this operation
categorySTRING NOT NULLHigh-level cost category: llm, external_api, event_processing, compute, storage
serviceSTRING NOT NULLService that incurred the cost: classification, reveal-company, edge-worker, identity-resolver, crm-processor, etc.
operationSTRING NOT NULLSpecific operation performed: kickfire_ip_lookup, page_classification, person_resolution_full, company_enrichment, etc.
unitsFLOAT64 NOT NULLQuantity consumed. Interpretation depends on unit_type: 1 (api_call or match), 1500 (tokens), 0.5 (cpu_seconds), etc.
unit_typeSTRING NOT NULLUnit of measure for the units field: api_call, tokens, cpu_ms, bytes, events, requests, match
platformSTRINGInfrastructure platform where the operation ran: gcp or cloudflare
regionSTRINGInfrastructure region: GCP region (e.g. us-central1) or Cloudflare colo code (e.g. DEN)
timestampTIMESTAMP NOT NULLWhen this operation occurred. Partition key
actual_cost_usdFLOAT64Actual total cost in USD for this operation when the service can compute it directly. NULL when cost must be derived by joining to cost_registry
actual_cost_per_unitFLOAT64Actual per-unit cost in USD when known. NULL when cost must be derived from cost_registry
input_tokensINT64LLM prompt/input token count. NULL for non-LLM operations
output_tokensINT64LLM completion/output token count. NULL for non-LLM operations
cached_tokensINT64LLM cached or reused token count (reduced cost tier). NULL for non-LLM operations
modelSTRINGLLM model name used for this operation, e.g. grok-4-fast, gemini-2.5-flash. Links to cost_registry.item for pricing. NULL for non-LLM operations
matchedBOOLFor per-match billing APIs (e.g. AtData): TRUE if the API call returned a successful match and incurred a match fee. FALSE if no match was found. NULL for non-match-billed operations
event_idSTRINGEvent identifier linking this usage to event_traces for pipeline context. NULL for batch operations such as ICP re-scoring or pruning runs
metadataJSONOperation-specific contextual details not covered by fixed columns, e.g. enrichment provider response codes, cache hit/miss context, batch size for bulk operations

⬡unlock_log

PII unlock audit log. Records each time anonymous visitor data is revealed through a consent transition. Tracks the multi-pass backfill execution (up to 3 passes with retry at +30 min and +60 min) used to retroactively associate previously anonymous records with the newly identified person. Internal operational table — NOT shared with customers. Source: consent processing pipeline (Plan 60e). Used for: privacy compliance audit trail, debugging unlock failures, monitoring backfill performance.

Partition: noneCluster: none
FieldTypeDescription
unlock_event_idSTRING NOT NULLUnique identifier for this unlock event
device_idSTRINGDevice ID associated with the unlock event
anonymous_idSTRINGAnonymous identifier that existed before PII resolution
transitionSTRINGIdentity transition type that triggered the unlock, e.g. anonymous_to_known, locked_to_unlocked
total_recordsINT64Total number of records eligible for backfill across all passes
pass1_atTIMESTAMPWhen pass 1 (immediate, runs at unlock time) executed
pass1_rows_updatedINT64Number of rows successfully updated in pass 1
pass1_duration_msINT64Duration of pass 1 execution in milliseconds
pass2_atTIMESTAMPWhen pass 2 (retry at +30 minutes) executed. NULL if pass 1 completed all rows
pass2_rows_updatedINT64Number of rows successfully updated in pass 2. NULL if pass 2 did not run
pass2_duration_msINT64Duration of pass 2 execution in milliseconds. NULL if pass 2 did not run
pass3_atTIMESTAMPWhen pass 3 (final retry at +60 minutes) executed. NULL if earlier passes completed all rows
pass3_rows_updatedINT64Number of rows successfully updated in pass 3. NULL if pass 3 did not run
pass3_duration_msINT64Duration of pass 3 execution in milliseconds. NULL if pass 3 did not run
statusSTRINGFinal processing status after all passes: complete (all rows updated), partial (some rows remain), or failed (processing error)
rows_remainingINT64Number of rows still unprocessed after all passes have run. 0 when status is complete
errorSTRINGError message if processing failed. NULL when status is complete or partial
created_atTIMESTAMPWhen this log entry was created. Defaults to CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
updated_atTIMESTAMPWhen this log entry was last updated. Defaults to CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
